#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,PartialEq,Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all="kebab-case")]
pub enum Reachability { AuthoredFixtureTrace, TrustedSeedOnly, SyntheticOperation }
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Counterexample {pub reachability:Reachability,pub invariant_site:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub viewer:String,pub operation:String,pub effect:String,pub row:Value,pub old:Option<Value>,pub supporting_rows:BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,pub rule_path:String,pub scope:String, pub observations:Option<paired::Observations>,pub history:Vec<histories::Step>}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Default,Serialize)]
pub struct Campaign {pub operations:BTreeMap<String,usize>,pub sequences:usize,pub attempted:usize,pub admitted:usize,pub refused:usize,pub violations:Vec<Counterexample>,pub boundary_failures:Vec<String>,pub unsupported:BTreeSet<String>}
fn id(table:&str)->Value {Value::Id(format!("security-{table}"))}
fn sample(kind:&C,variant:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match kind{
    C::Optional(inner)=>if variant&4!=0{sample(inner,variant).unwrap_or(Value::Null)}else{Value::Null},C::Id|C::Principal=>Value::Id(if variant&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()),
    C::Ref{table}=>id(table),C::Text{max}=>Value::String("x".repeat((*max).min(20) as usize)),
    C::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(variant%variants.len())?.clone()),
    C::Int|C::Decimal{..}|C::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),C::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),
    C::Bool=>Value::Bool((variant>>2)&1==0),C::Time=>Value::Int(NOW-1000),C::Json{..}=>Value::Null,C::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),
    C::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),C::ListRange{of,min,..}=>Value::Array((0..*min).map(|_|sample(of,variant)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    C::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),C::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),C::Video{..}=>return None,
})}
fn needed(schema:&Schema,name:&str,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){
    if !out.insert(name.into()){return;}let Some(t)=schema.table(name)else{return};
    for k in t.columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=k.required(){needed(schema,table,out);}}
    for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){needed(schema,&dep,out);}}
}
fn snapshot(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,false)
}
fn snapshot_mode(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize,timed:bool)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    let mut names=BTreeSet::new();needed(&bound.schema,&inv.table,&mut names);
    if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy{for dep in kernel::dependencies(policy){needed(&bound.schema,&dep,&mut names);}}
    if let Some(mut syntax)=inv.syntax.clone(){super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut syntax,&mut |e| {
        if let ast::Expr::Read(r)|ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=e {needed(&bound.schema,&r.table,&mut names);}
    });}
    if let Some(op)=&inv.operation && let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==op){for kind in p.program.args.values(){if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind{needed(&bound.schema,table,&mut names);}}}
    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();
    let mut enum_case=variant;
    for name in names {
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&name];
        if bound.schema.maintain_for(&name).is_some(){continue;}
        let mut fields=BTreeMap::new();fields.insert("id".into(),id(&name));
        for (column_index,(n,k)) in t.columns.iter().enumerate(){
            if n=="id"||t.engine_field(n)||t.maintained.contains_key(n){continue;}
            let sample_variant=if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){let n=enum_case % variants.len();enum_case/=variants.len();n}else if matches!(k,C::Principal){variant>>(3+column_index.min(4))}else{variant};
            let value=sample(k,sample_variant)
                .ok_or_else(||format!("{name}.{n}: asset ledger/required sample is not available"))?;
            fields.insert(n.clone(),value);
        }
        if timed || t.read_lifetime_sensitive() {for (n,k) in &t.columns {if matches!(k.required(),C::Time)&&(!k.is_optional()||variant&4!=0) {
            fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_ref()==Some(n){2000}else{-1000}));
        }}}
        for constraint in &t.constraints{if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=constraint
            && matches!(fields.get(field),Some(Value::String(_))) {fields.insert(field.clone(),Value::String("x".repeat((*min).max(1) as usize)));}}
        // Named primary keys used by role probes must select the challenged
        // viewer; changing the enum represents a current-role change while
        // derived assignment/membership rows remain present.
        if name!=inv.table {for (i,(n,k)) in t.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,C::Principal)).enumerate(){fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Id(if variant & (1 << (i+3)) == 0 {VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into())); let _=k;}}
        out.insert(name,vec![Value::Row(fields.into())]);
    }
    Ok(out)
}
struct Snapshot<'a>{schema:&'a Schema,rows:&'a BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>}
impl Probe for Snapshot<'_>{
    fn exists(&mut self,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[Value],equalities:&[(String,Value)])->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{
        let t=&self.schema.tables[table];let Some(idx)=t.index(index)else{return Ok(false)};
        if idx.components.len()!=key.len(){return Ok(false);}
        if idx.unique&&idx.components.iter().any(|c|c.column().is_some_and(|n|t.columns.get(n).is_some_and(C::is_optional)))&&key.contains(&Value::Null){return Ok(false);}
        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|idx.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(c,v)|c.column().is_some_and(|n|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v)))&&equalities.iter().all(|(n,v)|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v))))
    }
    fn created(&mut self,_:&str,_:&str,_:&[Value])->bool{false}
    // Policies describe raw independent intent. The observer itself must still
    // execute through the engine's live/authorized reference implementation.
    fn reference(&mut self,table:&str,id:&Value,field:&str)->Result<Option<Value>,RuntimeError>{
        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(id)).and_then(|r|r.field(field).ok()).cloned())
    }
}
fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->Result<bool,String>{
    
    let Some(p)=inv.policy.as_ref() else{return inv.syntax.as_ref().and_then(|e|super::concrete::evaluate(e,row,rows,&bound.schema,viewer)).ok_or_else(||"concrete policy expression is unknown/unsupported (including clock and typed literal coercions)".into());};
    
    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    rules::evaluate(p,viewer,old,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn program(name:&str,kind:ProgramKind,body:Vec<Stmt>)->Program{Program{name:name.into(),kind,args:BTreeMap::new(),body,read_time_granularity_ms:None,returned_shapes:BTreeMap::new(),native:false}}
fn engine(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probe:Program)->Result<Engine,String>{
    engine_with(bound,rows,vec![probe])
}
fn engine_with(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
    let store=Store::memory().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let mut programs=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();programs.extend(probes);
    let mut engine=Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),programs,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    fn hydrate(engine:&mut Engine,kind:&C,value:&Value)->Result<Value,String>{
        match (kind,value) {
            (C::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>hydrate(engine,inner,v),
            (C::List{of,..}|C::ListRange{of,..},Value::Array(xs))=>xs.iter().map(|v|hydrate(engine,of,v)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map(Value::Array),
            (C::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>engine.store_asset(VIEWER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|format!("verified image fixture: {e}")),
            (C::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(engine,VIEWER,types),
            _=>Ok(value.clone()),
        }
    }
    let mut seeded=rows.iter().filter(|(name,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(name).is_none()).map(|(name,rs)|{
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
        let clean=rs.iter().map(|r|if let Value::Row(fields)=r {Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect())}else{r.clone()}).collect();
        (name.clone(),clean)
    }).collect::<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>();
    for (table,values) in &mut seeded {for row in values {for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns {
        if let Ok(v)=row.field(column) {
            let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,v)?;
            if let Value::Row(fields)=row {fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}
        }
    }}}
    engine.seed_at(seeded.iter().flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>(),NOW).map_err(|e|format!("generated snapshot rejected by schema: {e}"))?;
    let actual=readback(&engine)?;
    for (name,values) in &actual {for row in values {
        snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_row(&bound.schema,name,row).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        for c in &bound.schema.tables[name].constraints {if let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=c {
            let rule=RuleExpr::Exists{table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),key:key.clone(),provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed,equalities:Vec::new()};
            if !rules::evaluate(&rule,VIEWER,None,row,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows:&actual}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())? {return Err(format!("{name}: trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"));}
        }}
    }}
    Ok(engine)
}
fn replay(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,word:&str,row:&Value)->Result<(bool,Value),String>{
    let name="$security.probe";let table=inv.table.clone();let key=id(&table);
    let read=Read{site:"security challenge".into(),table:table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(key.clone())],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed};
    let mut seeds=rows.clone();
    let probe=match word{
        "read"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(read))]),
        "insert"=>{seeds.remove(&table);program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Insert{table,row:Expr::Literal(row.clone())},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])},
        "delete"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Delete{table,key:Expr::Literal(key)},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]),
        "update"=>{
            let definition=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
            let changed=definition.columns.iter().filter(|(n,_)|n.as_str()!="id"&&!definition.engine_field(n))
                .filter(|(n,_)|inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|c==*n))
                .filter(|(n,_)|!definition.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(n))))
                .find_map(|(n,k)| {let old=row.field(n).ok()?;let next=match (k.required(),old){
                    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
                    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|v|Some(Value::String((*v).clone()))!=Some(old.clone()))?.clone()),
                    (C::Text{max},Value::String(v)) if v.chars().count()<*max as usize=>Value::String(format!("{v}x")),
                    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(v))=>Value::Int(v+1),_=>return None};
                    Some((n.clone(),next))});
            let Some((column,value))=changed else{return Err("no valid changed mutable-column sample".into());};
            program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Update{table,key:Expr::Literal(key),patch:Expr::Literal(Value::Map([(column,value)].into_iter().collect()))},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])
        },_=>return Err("row integrity/transition challenge is not an operation admission test".into()),
    };
    let mut engine=engine(bound,&seeds,probe)?;
    if word=="read"{return engine.query(name,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string());}
    let outcome=engine.mutate(name,None,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    if !matches!(outcome.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..}){return Ok((false,row.clone()));}
    let next=engine.store().rows(&inv.table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.into_iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
    if word=="delete"{return if next.is_none(){Ok((true,row.clone()))}else{Err("delete returned sent without removing the target".into())};}
    let next=next.ok_or("sent write has no committed target image")?;
    if word=="update" {
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let changed=table.columns.keys().filter(|c|!table.engine_field(c)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(*c))
            .any(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()&&inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|column|column==c));
        if !changed{return Err("sent no-op/implicit-only update has no changed declared target column".into());}
    }
    Ok((true,next))
}
fn readback(engine:&Engine)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{engine.schema().tables.keys().map(|t|engine.store().rows(t).map(|rs|(t.clone(),rs)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).collect()}

fn operation_preflight(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<(),String>{
    let Some(name)=&inv.operation else{return Ok(());};
    let p=&bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==name).ok_or("operation did not bind")?.program;
    if !super::census::body(&p.body){return Err(format!("{name}: opaque/loop/scan path requires write-boundary tracing"));}
    let rule=inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("operation policy outside rule fragment")?;
    if rule.any_term(&|t|!matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))){return Err("operation policy requires write-boundary row-image tracing".into());}
    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
    if writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).count()>1{return Err(format!("{name}: multiple target writes need executed-effect tracing; net images do not identify delete/reinsert"));}
    let unstable=kernel::dependencies(rule).iter().any(|dep|{
        let hits=writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==dep).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        !hits.is_empty()&&!(dep==&inv.table&&hits.len()==1&&hits[0].1=="insert")
    });
    if unstable||!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty()||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.maintained.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())){
        return Err("operation policy dependency may change before/after its write boundary; snapshot correspondence unproved".into());
    }Ok(())
}

/// Schema-only engine challenges. Successful synthetic operations refute the
/// declared table-authority contract, not application-operation reachability.
pub fn run(bound:&Bound)->Campaign{run_selected(bound,None)}
/// `check` searches unproved direct obligations; unsearched blockers remain
/// explicitly unproved. Default `test` passes None and runs the whole set.
pub fn run_selected(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>)->Campaign{
    let mut out=Campaign::default();
    // A direct component theorem needs no repeated concrete search for that
    // same component. Indirect observers, temporal reads and actual authored
    // operations below still run; the whole declaration remains unproved.
    let discharged=super::analyze(bound).invariants.into_iter()
        .filter(|v|v.direct_rule==Status::Proved&&v.invariant.operation.is_none()
            && (matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"|"insert"|"delete")
                || matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"update"|"write")&&bound.schema.tables[&v.invariant.table].written_by.is_empty()))
        .map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut assertions=bound.invariants.clone();
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.is_some(){continue;}
        if inv.operation.is_some()||matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"){continue;}
        for p in &bound.programs {if p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
            let relevant=ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==&inv.table&&(inv.target=="write"||inv.target==*kind||*kind=="upsert"));
            if relevant{let mut op=inv.clone();op.operation=Some(p.program.name.clone());assertions.push(op);}
        }
    }
    for inv in &assertions{
        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.is_some(){continue;}
        if sites.is_some_and(|sites|!sites.contains(&inv.site.to_string())){continue;}
        if inv.operation.is_none()&&discharged.contains(&inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
        if let Err(why)=operation_preflight(bound,inv){out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}
        let mut found=false;
        let combinations=bound.schema.tables.values().flat_map(|t|t.columns.values()).filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).fold(1usize,usize::saturating_mul).max(24);
        if combinations>512{out.unsupported.insert("schema enum Cartesian matrix exceeds 512 samples; remaining tuples are not covered".into());}
        for sample in 0..=combinations.min(512) {
            let variant=sample.saturating_sub(1);
            let mut rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
            // Try explicit policy field literals before the unchanged enum
            // matrix. A value such as status='cancelled' need not wait behind
//! Paired logical observations, replayed independently in memory.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — two valid worlds and repeatable observations are required.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Observations {
    pub present: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent_rows: BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,
}

pub(super) fn observe(engine:&mut Engine,p:&Program,viewer:&str,args:BTreeMap<String,Value>)->Result<serde_json::Value,String>{
    let context=Context::new(viewer,args).at(NOW);
    if p.kind==ProgramKind::Query {
        match engine.query(&p.name,context) {
            Ok(q) if q.unavailable || !q.complete => Err("incomplete/metered query is outside logical payload comparison".into()),
            Ok(q)=>serde_json::to_value(&q.data).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
            Err(e)=>Err(format!("query error is not an admitted observation: {e}")),
        }
    }else{
        match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,context).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.state {
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{result,..}=>Ok(serde_json::json!({"sent":result})),
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{why,current}=>{
                // E_RULE can also mask authorization-budget exhaustion. Only
                // an authored require/constraint carries this rule marker;
                // bound/storage/schema/ambiguous engine refusals cannot be a
                // logical counterexample in our resource-excluding contract.
                if why.rule.is_none()||!matches!(why.family.as_str(),"rule"|"constraint") {
                    return Err(format!("resource/engine or ambiguous mutation refusal {} is outside paired logical comparison",why.code));
                }
                Ok(serde_json::json!({"refusal":{"code":why.code,"message":why.message},"current":current}))
            },
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
        if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation needs complete bound policy dependencies; unsupported policy AST is not dependency-free",inv.table));continue;}
        if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
        // Closed transitive observer set: include maintained targets, inherited
        // readers and every table whose rule probes an affected population.
        // An operation mentioning none of them has no logical input changed
        // by this pair. This prunes experiments, never proves an obligation.
        let mut observers=BTreeSet::from([inv.table.clone()]);
        loop {
            let before=observers.len();
            for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if observers.contains(&m.source){
                observers.insert(m.target.clone());
                if let Some(owner)=&m.private{observers.insert(owner.table.clone());}
            }}
            for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables {
                if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|observers.contains(&p.parent))
                    || [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|d|observers.contains(d))) {
                    observers.insert(name.clone());
                }
            }
            if observers.len()==before{break;}
        }
        // Removing an ordinary row must not manufacture an invalid reference
        // graph. More general paired deletions need a checked closure.
        if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))) {
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired absence needs a referentially valid world closure",inv.table));continue;
        }
        for variant in 0..8 {
            let Ok(present)=snapshot(bound,inv,variant)else{continue;};
            if present.keys().any(|name|bound.schema.tables[name].columns.values().any(C::contains_asset)) {
                out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired fixture asset identities are not coupled",inv.table));continue;
            }
            let Some(row)=present.get(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first()).cloned()else{continue;};
            let mut absent=present.clone();absent.remove(&inv.table);
            // Final-exists validation is a pure judgment of this fixed seeded
            // world, before operation/viewer admission. Retain its rejection
            // across observers, never cache execution/resource/policy results.
            let invalid=std::cell::RefCell::new([None::<String>,None]);
            for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER,"account:security-no-role","service:security"] {
                for bp in &bound.programs {
                    let p=&bp.program;
                    if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native {continue;}
                    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
                    let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                    if writes.iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==&inv.table||deps.contains(table)||*kind!="update")
                        || !writes.is_empty() && (!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty() || bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.on_delete.is_empty())) {
                        out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired mutation lacks stable policy/implicit-write and deterministic-id reduction",p.name));continue;
                    }
                    let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();
                    if !observers.iter().any(|table|super::super::audit::mentions(&json,table)){continue;}
                    // Fresh generated ids and opaque viewer/work contexts are
                    // not repeatable functions of these paired inputs.
                    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool {match v{
                        serde_json::Value::String(s)=>s=="NewId"||s=="JobAs",
                        serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"Emit"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"))||m.values().any(opaque),
                        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false,
                    }}
                    if opaque(&json){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque effects excluded from paired determinism",p.name));continue;}
                    let Some(args)=arguments(&p.args,variant)else{continue;};
                    let execute=|seeds:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>|->Result<(serde_json::Value,BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>),String>{
                        let slot=usize::from(std::ptr::eq(seeds,&absent));
                        if let Some(why)=&invalid.borrow()[slot]{return Err(why.clone());}
                        let mut e=match engine(bound,seeds,program("$security.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){
                            Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{
                                if why.ends_with(": trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"){invalid.borrow_mut()[slot]=Some(why.clone());}
                                return Err(why);
                            },
                        };
                        let actual=readback(&e)?;
                        // Judge the hypothetical protected row in each complete
                        // committed world, before any operation can change roles.
                        if policy(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,&row)!=Ok(false){return Err("paired policy is not definitely false in both worlds".into());}
                        observe(&mut e,p,viewer,args.clone()).map(|value|(value,actual))
                    };
                    let ((a,rows),(b,absent_rows))=match (execute(&present),execute(&absent)) {
                        (Ok(a),Ok(b))=>(a,b),
                        (Err(e),_)|(_,Err(e))=>{
                            if !e.starts_with("paired policy is not definitely false") {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.name));}
                            continue;
                        },
                    };
                    out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
                    if a==b {continue;}
                    // The query order is engine-defined. Recreate both worlds
                    // to decline store-scoped cursors or unstable outputs.
                    if !matches!((execute(&present),execute(&absent)),(Ok((aa,_)),Ok((bb,_))) if aa==a&&bb==b){
                        out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation is not repeatable",p.name));continue;
                    }
                    out.violations.push(Counterexample {reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.into(),
                        operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"read observation".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:rows,
                        rule_path:"paired present/absent engine executions differ while the declared privacy policy is false in both".into(),
                        scope:"schema-valid seed-ingress paired worlds; authenticated logical data/refusal only; no application reachability claim".into(),
                        observations:Some(Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows})});
                    break;
                }
                if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.observations.is_some()){break;}
            }
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.observations.is_some()){break;}
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("paired observations exclude wire sequences/receipts, physical timing and resource/meter differences; HTTP and device histories require separate qualification".into());
}
//! The same fixture loader serves query testing and residency at either CLI entry point.
use super::Options;
use crate::project::Project;
use snapback4_core::{Engine, wire};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;

pub(super) fn engine(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    engine_at(project, bound, options, snapback4_core::now_ms())
}

pub(super) fn engine_at(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options, now: i64) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture") else {
        if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            return Engine::new_at(store, bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
        }
        return project.open_engine(bound).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open the store: {error}"));
    };
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut schema = bound.schema.clone();
    // Residency defers ordinary maintains until after bulk loading and builds
    // them once; column derives stay installed and maintain as rows insert.
    let residency = options.flags.contains_key("residency");
    if residency { schema.maintains.retain(|m| m.private.is_some()); }
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut engine = Engine::new_at(store, schema, bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(fixture).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {fixture}: {error}"))? {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {fixture}: {error}"))?;
        names.push(entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string());
    }
    names.sort();
    // One batch for the whole directory: the engine orders parents before
    // children within a batch, so file order (and read_dir order) cannot make
    // a read-through child or a reference miss its parent.
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    let mut unknown = Vec::new();
    for name in names {
        let Some(table) = name.strip_suffix(".jsonl") else { continue };
        if name == super::test_cases::FILE { continue; }
        let path = std::path::Path::new(fixture).join(&name);
        let Some(definition) = bound.schema.tables.get(table) else {
            if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") {
                eprintln!("test: skipped {} — saved source declares no table {table}; pass --fixture <dir> to select contemporary fixtures explicitly", path.display());
                continue;
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — the starter's own unmodified sample is skipped loudly; a project's file for a missing table still refuses.
            if !options.flags.contains_key("source") && super::init::starter_fixture(&name).is_some_and(|starter| std::fs::read_to_string(&path).is_ok_and(|text| text == starter)) {
                eprintln!("test: WARNING: skipped {} — the todo starter's unmodified sample rows for a `{table}` table your snapback/*.q does not declare; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's other leftovers", path.display());
                continue;
            }
            unknown.push(name);
            continue;
        };
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture file {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
        for (line_number, line) in text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_, line)| !line.trim().is_empty()) {
            let at = format!("{name}:{}", line_number + 1);
            let mut json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}"))?;
            // A residency fixture is often an exported store: engine-maintained
            // columns are dropped and rebuilt from the source rows. `test`
            // refuses them as an insert and `/seed` do (E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN).
            if residency && let Some(fields) = json.as_object_mut() { fields.retain(|field, _| !definition.maintained.contains_key(field)); }
            relative_times(definition, &mut json, now).map_err(|why| format!("{at}: {why}"))?;
            if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &json) { return Err(format!("{at}: {why}")); }
            let row = wire::row_from_json(definition, &json).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}\n  hint: a fixture row is one JSON object per line with the table's fields and an \"id\"; see snapback4 guide testing"))?;
            rows.push((table.to_owned(), row));
        }
    }
    // A misspelled file name would otherwise load nothing and still pass.
    if !unknown.is_empty() {
        let tables: Vec<&str> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
        // 0.4.17's init wrote the todo starter's rows beside a project's own schema.
        let starter = unknown.iter().any(|name| name == "todos.jsonl")
            && std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(fixture).join("todos.jsonl")).is_ok_and(|text| text.contains("\"id\":\"todo-milk\""));
        let starter = if starter { format!("\n  hint: todos.jsonl is the todo starter's sample that `snapback4 init` wrote; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's {} while they are unmodified (or delete them, and the starter's list/add/toggle/remove lines, yourself)", super::test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
        return Err(format!("{fixture}: {} name{} no table in snapback/*.q; nothing was run{starter}\n  hint: a fixture file is <table>.jsonl for a declared table ({}), plus {}; see snapback4 guide testing",
            unknown.join(", "), if unknown.len() == 1 { "s" } else { "" }, tables.join(", "), super::test_cases::FILE));
    }
    // Fixture rows take `/seed`'s insert path (defaults, sequences, unique with the author's codes, caps, maintained views), not the trusted bulk import.
    let loaded = engine.seed_at(rows, now).map_err(|error| {
        let text = error.to_string();
        let hint = if text.contains("NOT_FOUND") { "\n  hint: every fixture file loads as one batch, parents first; NOT_FOUND means a referenced or read-through parent row is in no fixture file (check the id)" } else { "" };
        format!("{fixture}: {text}{hint}")
    })?;
    if residency {
        engine.adopt_at(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot build fixture maintains: {error}"))?;
    }
    if !options.json { println!("test: loaded {loaded} rows from {fixture} in {:.1} s", started.elapsed().as_secs_f64()); }
    Ok(engine)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#5-fixtures-and-dev-evidence — relative time leaves, seeds and operations share one captured clock.
/// Only typed time leaves interpret `now`: text and JSON retain their literals.
pub(crate) fn relative_times(table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table, row: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = row.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(name) { time_value(kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?; }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn time_value(kind: &snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType, value: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) if !value.is_null() => time_value(inner, value, now),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, serde_json::Value::Array(items)) => {
            for item in items { time_value(of, item, now)?; }
            Ok(())
        }
        (ColumnType::Time, value) => {
            let Some(text) = value.as_str().filter(|text| text.starts_with("now")) else { return Ok(()) };
            let delta = if text == "now" { 0 } else {
                let offset = text.strip_prefix("now").unwrap();
                if !offset.starts_with(['+', '-']) { return Err(format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?}: expected now or now±duration (ms, s, m, h, d, w)")); }
                crate::dev_clock::offset(offset).map_err(|why| format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?}: {}", why.message))?
            };
            let resolved = now.checked_add(delta).filter(|time| (0..=snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX).contains(time))
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?} is outside Unix milliseconds 0..9007199254740991"))?;
            *value = resolved.into();
            Ok(())
        }
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}
pub(super) fn argument_times(kinds: &std::collections::BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::ir::ArgType>, args: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = args.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = kinds.get(name).and_then(snapback4_core::schema::arg_as_column) {
                time_value(&kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod column_tests {
    use super::*;
    use snapback4_core::{Context, Value};
    #[test]
    fn residency_fixture_import_rebuilds_poisoned_columns() {
        let source=include_str!("../../../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/maintained-columns.q");
        let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty());
        let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("f1a-fixture-{}",std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        {
            let files=[("posts",r#"{"id":"p","at":1,"likeCount":999,"selected":999,"total":999}"#),
                ("likes",r#"{"id":"l","postId":"p","active":true,"amount":7}"#)];
            for index in [0,1] {std::fs::write(root.join(format!("{}.jsonl",files[index].0)),files[index].1).unwrap();}
            let options=Options {positionals:vec![],json:true,flags:[("fixture".into(),root.to_str().unwrap().into()),("residency".into(),String::new())].into()};
            let project=Project {root:root.clone()};
            let mut engine=engine(&project,&bound,&options).unwrap();
            let row=engine.query("postRow",Context::new("alice",[("id".into(),Value::Id("p".into()))].into()).at(1000)).unwrap().data;
            assert_eq!(["likeCount","selected","total"].map(|field|row.field(field).unwrap().as_int().unwrap()),[1,1,7]);
            assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("posts").unwrap(),vec![row]);
            for m in &engine.schema().maintains {
                let rows=engine.store().rows(&m.target).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(rows.len(),1);
                assert_eq!(rows[0].field("value").unwrap(),&Value::Int(if m.private.as_ref().unwrap().column=="total" {7} else {1}));
            }
            for (table,_) in files {std::fs::remove_file(root.join(format!("{table}.jsonl"))).unwrap();}
        }
        std::fs::remove_dir(&root).unwrap();
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod order_tests {
    use super::*;
    // `items` sorts before `lists`: the child's file is read first, yet the
    // directory loads as one batch with parents ordered before children.
    #[test]
    fn read_through_child_file_before_parent_file_loads() {
        let source = "use personas alice, bob\n\ntable lists:\n  owner: principal\n  title: text 1..80\n  immutable owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  cap 50 by owner\n  sync to .owner\n\ntable items:\n  parent: lists\n  text: text 1..200\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  insert <- .parent.owner = viewer\n  cap 50 by parent\n";
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("fixture-order-{}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(root.join("items.jsonl"), "{\"id\":\"00000000000000000000000C01\",\"parent\":\"00000000000000000000000B01\",\"text\":\"wash\"}\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(root.join("lists.jsonl"), "{\"id\":\"00000000000000000000000B01\",\"owner\":\"dev:alice\",\"title\":\"Home\"}\n").unwrap();
        let options = Options { positionals: vec![], json: true, flags: [("fixture".into(), root.to_str().unwrap().into())].into() };
        let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        let engine = engine(&project, &bound, &options);
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let engine = engine.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("items").unwrap().len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("lists").unwrap().len(), 1);
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod relative_tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;
    #[test]
    fn relative_time_is_typed_checked_and_does_not_rewrite_text_or_json() {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table notes:\n  at: time\n  optionalAt: time ?\n  title: text <=80\n  payload: json <=200\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut table = bound.schema.tables["notes"].clone();
        use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
        table.columns.insert("times".into(), ColumnType::List { of: Box::new(ColumnType::Optional(Box::new(ColumnType::Time))), max: 4 });
        let now = 1_000_000_000;
        for (text, expected) in [("now", now), ("now-1h", now-3_600_000), ("now+2d", now+172_800_000), ("now+1ms", now+1)] {
            let mut row = json!({"at":text, "optionalAt":text, "title":"now-1h", "payload":{"time":"now"}});
            relative_times(&table, &mut row, now).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(row, json!({"at":expected,"optionalAt":expected,"title":"now-1h","payload":{"time":"now"}}));
        }
        for text in ["nowsoon", "now+0h", "now+1.5h", "now-9999999999999999999h", "now-100w"] {
            let error = relative_times(&table, &mut json!({"at":text}), now).unwrap_err();
            assert!(error.contains("E_FIXTURE_TIME") && error.contains(text), "{error}");
        }
        let mut null = json!({"at":42,"optionalAt":null});
        relative_times(&table, &mut null, now).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(null, json!({"at":42,"optionalAt":null}));
        let mut list = json!({"times":["now", null, "now-1s"]});
        relative_times(&table, &mut list, now).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(list, json!({"times":[now, null, now-1000]}));
    }
    #[test]
    fn seed_defaults_and_relative_rows_share_the_captured_clock() {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table notes:\n  at: time default now\n  ends: time\n  expire at .ends\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nquery one():\n  return notes[\"n\"]\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("rel3-relative-{}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(root.join("notes.jsonl"), r#"{"id":"n","ends":"now+1ms"}"#).unwrap();
        let options = Options { positionals: vec![], json: true, flags: [("fixture".into(),root.to_str().unwrap().into())].into() };
        let now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
        let mut engine = engine_at(&Project { root:root.clone() }, &bound, &options, now).unwrap();
        let row = engine.query("one", snapback4_core::Context::new("dev:alice", Default::default()).at(now)).unwrap().data;
        assert_eq!(row.field("at").unwrap(), &snapback4_core::Value::Int(now));
        assert_eq!(row.field("ends").unwrap(), &snapback4_core::Value::Int(now+1));
        let gone = engine.query("one", snapback4_core::Context::new("dev:alice", Default::default()).at(now+1)).unwrap().data;
        assert_eq!(gone, snapback4_core::Value::Null);
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
}

//! Paired logical observations, replayed independently in memory.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — two valid worlds and repeatable observations are required.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Observations {
    pub present: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent_rows: BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,
}

pub(super) fn observe(engine:&mut Engine,p:&Program,viewer:&str,args:BTreeMap<String,Value>)->Result<serde_json::Value,String>{
    let context=Context::new(viewer,args).at(NOW);
    if p.kind==ProgramKind::Query {
        match engine.query(&p.name,context) {
            Ok(q) if q.unavailable || !q.complete => Err("incomplete/metered query is outside logical payload comparison".into()),
            Ok(q)=>serde_json::to_value(&q.data).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
            Err(e)=>Err(format!("query error is not an admitted observation: {e}")),
        }
    }else{
        match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,context).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.state {
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{result,..}=>Ok(serde_json::json!({"sent":result})),
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{why,current}=>{
                // E_RULE can also mask authorization-budget exhaustion. Only
                // an authored require/constraint carries this rule marker;
                // bound/storage/schema/ambiguous engine refusals cannot be a
                // logical counterexample in our resource-excluding contract.
                if why.rule.is_none()||!matches!(why.family.as_str(),"rule"|"constraint") {
                    return Err(format!("resource/engine or ambiguous mutation refusal {} is outside paired logical comparison",why.code));
                }
                Ok(serde_json::json!({"refusal":{"code":why.code,"message":why.message},"current":current}))
            },
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
        if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation needs complete bound policy dependencies; unsupported policy AST is not dependency-free",inv.table));continue;}
        if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
//! Logical observer obligations, separate from the whole declaration verdict.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — pull group authority back to each contributor.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Obligation {
    pub path: String,
    pub status: Status,
    pub reason: String,
    pub repair: Option<String>,
}
impl Obligation {
    fn implication(path: String, admission: Result<RuleExpr, String>, policy: &RuleExpr) -> Self {
        let result = admission.and_then(|a| kernel::implies(&a, policy));
        let (status, reason, repair) = match result {
            Ok(true) => (Status::Proved, "every admitted logical contributor satisfies this policy".into(), None),
            Ok(false) => (Status::Unproved, "observer authority does not imply the source policy".into(),
                Some("retain the privacy fields as bare group keys and apply the source privacy policy to this observer".into())),
            Err(why) => (Status::Unproved, why, Some("use ordinary group keys and a read rule over those keys; keep opaque/temporal derivations private".into())),
        };
        Self { path, status, reason, repair }
    }
}

pub(super) fn obligations(bound: &Bound, inv: &Invariant) -> Vec<Obligation> {
    if inv.target != "read" { return Vec::new(); }
    let Some(policy) = &inv.policy else { return Vec::new(); };
    let table = &bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
    let mut out = vec![Obligation::implication(
        "server point/scan/planned view/count/sum/exists payload".into(),
        Ok(super::columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())), policy)];
    for m in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.source == inv.table) {
        let admission = (|| {
            if m.row_key.is_some() || m.parent_key.is_some() || m.private.is_some()
                || !m.aliases.is_empty() || !m.computed.is_empty() || m.own_keys.is_some()
                || table.read_lifetime_sensitive()
            { return Err(format!("{} -> {}: captured/private/computed/temporal maintenance needs a contributor reduction", m.source, m.target)); }
            let target = bound.schema.table(&m.target).ok_or_else(|| format!("{}: maintained-column observer needs its owning table/field authority", m.target))?;
            if m.by.iter().any(|key|key.contains('.')||key.starts_with('$')||table.recorded.contains_key(key)
                || table.columns.get(key).is_none() || table.columns.get(key)!=target.columns.get(key)) {
                return Err(format!("{} -> {}: group keys are not bare identically typed source/target columns",m.source,m.target));
            }
            if target.through.is_some() || target.read_lifetime_sensitive() {
                return Err(format!("{}: derived observer has inherited/temporal authority", m.target));
            }
            // A target group field equals that field on every contributing
            // source row. Its id/value and all lost fields have no such law.
            pullback(&target.rules.read, &m.by)
        })();
        out.push(Obligation::implication(format!("maintained {:?}: {} -> {}", m.kind, m.source, m.target), admission, policy));
        for next in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|n| n.source == m.target) {
            out.push(Obligation { path: format!("derived chain {} -> {} -> {}", inv.table, m.target, next.target),
                status: Status::Unproved, reason: "downstream contributor substitution is not implemented".into(),
                repair: Some("query the directly protected aggregate, or declare and verify privacy on each derived observer".into()) });
        }
    }
    out
}

fn pullback(rule: &RuleExpr, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleExpr, String> {
    fn term(t: &RuleTerm, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleTerm, String> {
        match t {
            RuleTerm::Viewer | RuleTerm::Literal(_) => Ok(t.clone()),
            RuleTerm::Field(f) if keys.contains(f) => Ok(t.clone()),
            _ => Err("derived rule reads a value/id/lost group field or a reference without a contributor identity".into()),
        }
    }
    Ok(match rule {
        RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny | RuleExpr::Public {..} => rule.clone(),
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) => RuleExpr::Eq(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) => RuleExpr::Ne(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b) => RuleExpr::Compare(*op,term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::And(xs) => RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        RuleExpr::Or(xs) => RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        RuleExpr::Not(x) => RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(pullback(x,keys)?)),
        RuleExpr::Exists {table,index,key,provenance,equalities} => RuleExpr::Exists {table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),
            key:key.iter().map(|t|term(t,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?,provenance:*provenance,
            equalities:equalities.iter().map(|(n,t)|Ok((n.clone(),term(t,keys)?))).collect::<Result<_,String>>()?},
        RuleExpr::Created {..} => return Err("created probe has no derived read contributor reduction".into()),
    })
}
//! Real upload ledgers for foreign-id and non-image placement challenges.
// @ref LLP 3000.038#3-upload — assets retain upload ownership and verified media type.
use super::*;
const PDF:&[u8]=b"%PDF-1.4\n%%EOF\n";
pub(super) fn file(e:&mut Engine,owner:&str,types:&[String])->Result<Value,String>{
    let mime=types.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("application/pdf");
    let (bytes,name)=match mime{"application/pdf"=>(PDF,"proof.pdf"),"text/plain"=>(b"verified text\n".as_slice(),"proof.txt"),"image/png"=>(PNG.as_slice(),"proof.png"),_=>return Err(format!("file ledger needs a verified {mime} fixture"))};
    e.store_file(owner,bytes,Some(mime),name,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn contains(value:&Value,id:&Value)->bool {
    if value==id{return true;}
    match value{Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Map(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Row(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    if initial.values().any(|xs|!xs.is_empty()){
        out.unsupported.insert("foreign-asset replay over populated fixtures needs full ledger transfer; empty-ledger challenges run separately".into());
    }
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        let p=&bp.program;
        for (arg,kind) in &p.args{
            if !matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}|ir::ArgType::File{..}){continue;}
            for mode in ["foreign upload","non-image photo"]{
                if mode=="non-image photo"&&!matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}){continue;}
                let mut e=match engine(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),program("$security.asset",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let sample=if mode=="non-image photo"{file(&mut e,VIEWER,&[])}else{match kind{
                    ir::ArgType::Image{..}=>e.store_asset(OTHER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
                    ir::ArgType::File{types,..}=>file(&mut e,OTHER,types),_=>unreachable!(),
                }};
                let sample=match sample{Ok(v)=>v,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let Some(mut args)=arguments(&p.args,0)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge needs remaining argument fixtures",p.name));continue;};
                args.insert(arg.clone(),sample.clone());
                out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                match e.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(VIEWER,args).at(NOW)){
                    Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>out.refused+=1,
                    Ok(_)=>{if let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)&&rows.values().flatten().any(|row|contains(row,&sample)){
                        out.boundary_failures.push(format!("{} placed {mode} through argument {arg}",p.name));
                    }},
                    Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge engine error {error}",p.name));},
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
//! Saved-source fixture selection, business cases and the query sweep.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#9-wave-13-saved-version-proofs-day3-2026-10-16 — contemporary fixtures belong to the selected saved source.
use super::*;

const CASE_SUMMARY: &str = "SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES";

fn case_count(path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<usize, String> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
    Ok(text.lines().filter(|line| {
        let line = line.trim();
        !line.is_empty() && !line.starts_with("//")
    }).count())
}

/// `test`: run every argument-free query as each persona against the store
/// (optionally loaded from a fixture) and print the meters beside the
/// ceilings, so a builder reads numbers instead of being refused on a bound.
pub fn test(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    let project = match proof_source::project(options, "source") { Ok(project) => project, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let sources = match proof_source::sources(&project, options, "source") { Ok(sources) => sources, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let bound = compiled_or_exit(proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "source"), options.json);
    let mut defaults = options.clone();
    let mut skipped_business_cases = 0;
    if !defaults.flags.contains_key("fixture") {
        let saved = options.flags.get("source").map(std::path::Path::new).map(|path| {
            if path.is_dir() { path.join("fixtures") }
            else if path.file_name().is_some_and(|name| name == "schema.q") { path.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures") }
            else { path.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures", path.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy())) }
        });
        if let Some(fixture) = saved.filter(|path| path.is_dir()) {
            eprintln!("test: using {} (saved version's contemporary fixtures; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display());
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir() {
            let fixture = project.root.join("fixtures");
            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
                defaults.flags.insert("saved-fixture-fallback".into(), String::new());
                eprintln!("test: no contemporary fixtures for saved source; validating only declared-table files in {}; pass --fixture <dir> for a complete contemporary case suite", fixture.display());
                let cases = fixture.join(test_cases::FILE);
                if cases.is_file() {
                    skipped_business_cases = match case_count(&cases) { Ok(count) => count, Err(error) => { eprintln!("test: {error}"); return 1; } };
                    eprintln!("test: skipped {} — working cases may target another release; pass --fixture <dir> to run explicitly", cases.display());
                }
            } else { eprintln!("test: using {} (default fixture directory; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display()); }
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            eprintln!("test: no saved or working fixtures; query sweep only; pass --fixture <dir> for business cases");
        }
    }
    let options = &defaults;
    // test applies dev's cast: a persona dev would refuse is not run as an ordinary viewer.
    // Residency measures any principal in the data (scale fixtures name thousands), so it notes instead.
    if let Some(why) = options.flags.get("as").and_then(|names| names.split(',').find_map(|name| test_cases::undeclared_persona(name, &bound.personas))) {
        if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
            eprintln!("test --as: {why}");
            return 1;
        }
        eprintln!("test --residency --as: measuring an undeclared principal; dev would refuse it as a persona");
    }
    let _owner = if options.flags.contains_key("fixture") || options.flags.contains_key("source") { None } else {
        match crate::store_owner::command(&project.store_path()) {
            Ok(owner) => Some(owner),
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    };
    let fixture_now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let opened = match &_owner {
        Some(owner) => owner.open_engine(&bound, std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), fixture_now).map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None => test_fixture::engine_at(&project, &bound, options, fixture_now),
    };
    let mut engine = match opened {
        Ok(engine) => engine,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
    };
    let personas = residency::personas(&bound, options);
    let sweep_personas = if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") { Default::default() } else {
        match test_sweep::load(options.flags.get("fixture"), &bound) {
            Ok(matrix) => matrix,
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    };
    if let Some(persona) = options.flags.get("as") && !sweep_personas.is_empty() {
        println!("test --as {persona} overrides test.sweep.json because --as requests one deliberate global probe; for: {}", sweep_personas.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
        let verbose = options.flags.contains_key("verbose");
        if let Err(error) = residency::measure(&mut engine, &bound, &personas, verbose) { eprintln!("residency measurement failed: {error}"); return 1; }
        if !verbose {
            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
                println!("{CASE_SUMMARY} {{\"run\":0,\"skipped\":{skipped_business_cases}}}");
            }
            if !options.flags.contains_key("fixture-only") {
                eprintln!("test --residency executed no business cases; use --fixture-only for acquisition measurement, or omit --residency to run the cases");
                return 1;
            }
            return 0;
        }
    }
    let mut security_campaign = snapback4_lang::security::challenges::run(&bound);
    snapback4_lang::security::challenges::operations(&bound,&engine,&mut security_campaign);
    let security_http_ok=security::unauthenticated(&bound,&sources);
    if security_http_ok{security_campaign.unsupported.retain(|s|!s.starts_with("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require"));}
    println!("security histories: {} ordered short operation sequences replayed",security_campaign.sequences);
    for why in &security_campaign.boundary_failures {println!("security boundary failure: {why}");}
    println!("security operation matrix: {} operations exercised; unknown authorization intent is not inferred from success",security_campaign.operations.len());
    println!("security tests: {} attempted, {} refused, {} violations; finite campaign, not proof",security_campaign.attempted,security_campaign.refused,security_campaign.violations.len());
    for c in security_campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|options.flags.contains_key("all")||c.reachability==snapback4_lang::security::challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace) {println!("security violation: {}",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap());}
    for reason in &security_campaign.unsupported {println!("security not covered: {reason}");}
    let (mut passed, mut failed_cases) = (0, 0);
    if let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_| !options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback")) {
        match test_cases::run_verbose(&mut engine, &bound, fixture, &sources, fixture_now, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")) {
            Ok((p, f)) => { passed = p; failed_cases = f; }
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    }
    let ceilings = engine.ceilings();
