                                if inv.target=="update"&&old.is_none() || inv.target=="insert"&&old.is_some() || inv.target=="delete" {continue;}
                                if let Some(column)=&inv.column && old.is_some_and(|old|old.field(column).ok()==next.field(column).ok()){continue;}
                                if permitted==Ok(false){out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:operation.clone(),effect:if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"}.into(),row:next.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:rows.clone(),rule_path:format!("authored operation {operation}"),scope:"actual authored mutation committed a changed image from a schema-valid seeded snapshot".into()});found=true;break;}
                            }
                        },_=>out.refused+=1,
                    }
                    if found{break;}continue;
                }
                for word in super::effects(inv){
                    let mut admission_rows=rows.clone();if word=="insert"{admission_rows.remove(&inv.table);}
                    let allowed=match policy(bound,inv,&admission_rows,viewer,(word!="insert").then_some(row),row){Ok(v)=>v,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                    if allowed{continue;}out.attempted+=1;
                    let replayed=if word=="read" {seeded.query("$security.seed",Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string())}else{replay(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,word,row)};
                    match replayed{
                        Ok((false,_))=>out.refused+=1,
                        Ok((true,next))=>{
                            let mut args=BTreeMap::new();
                            if word=="update"{let changes=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.keys().filter(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()).filter_map(|c|next.field(c).ok().map(|v|(c.clone(),v.clone()))).collect();args.insert("$syntheticPatch".into(),Value::Map(changes));}
                            out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args,viewer:viewer.into(),operation:format!("$security.{}.{}",inv.table,word),effect:word.into(),row:next,old:(word=="update"||word=="delete").then(||row.clone()),supporting_rows:admission_rows.clone(),rule_path:bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].rules.for_effect(super::effect(word)).quarry(),scope:if word=="update"{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; old-image policy false at an actual changed update; committed next image and patch shown; application reachability is not claimed".into()}else{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; synthetic engine table operation; application reachability is not claimed".into()}});found=true;break;},
                        Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: {e}",inv.table,word));},
                    }
                }
                if found{break;}
            }
            if found{break;}
        }
    }
    column_reads::generated(bound,&mut out,sites);
    paired::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    timed::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    out.unsupported.insert("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require a live server; engine principals are authenticated contexts".into());
    out.unsupported.insert("offline/revoked-device access and retained history require device boundary qualification".into());out
}
pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());for c in &campaign.violations{for v in &mut report.invariants{if v.invariant.site.to_string()==c.invariant_site{
    if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|old.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace)&&c.reachability!=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace{continue;}
    v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; finite test evidence, not a reachability theorem".into());
}}}}

fn arguments(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,variant:usize)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    use ir::ArgType as A;
    fn one(k:&A,v:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match k{
        A::Ref{table}=>id(table),A::Principal=>Value::Id(if v&1==0{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),A::Id=>Value::Id("security-argument".into()),A::Cursor|A::Optional(_)=>Value::Null,
        A::Text{min,max,..}=>Value::String("z".repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize)),A::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(v%variants.len())?.clone()),A::Int|A::Decimal{..}|A::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),A::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),A::Time=>Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000),A::Bool=>Value::Bool(v&2==0),A::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),A::Json{..}=>Value::Null,A::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),A::Image{..}|A::File{..}=>Value::Null,_=>return None,
    })}
    args.iter().map(|(n,k)|one(k,variant).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
}

/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rows) in &fixtures{for row in rows{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!viewers.contains(p){viewers.push(p.clone());}}}}
    for p in &bound.programs{
        if !matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.program.native{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: trusted/system/opaque operation",p.program.name));continue;}
        let target=bound.schema.visible_tables().find(|(t,_)|super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t)).or_else(||bound.schema.visible_tables().next()).map(|(t,_)|t.clone());
        let Some(target)=target else{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no table population for generated arguments",p.program.name));continue;};
        Ok(outcome)
    }

    /// Write rows as the system principal: rules are bypassed, validation,
    /// constraints, aggregates and partition events are not. One commit.
    pub fn seed(&mut self, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.seed_at(rows, crate::now_ms())
    }

    /// Development hosts supply their clock so seed commits share operation and stream time.
    pub fn seed_at(&mut self, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>, now: i64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        let now = self.advance_time_audience(now)?;
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let now = time_audience::pin_writer_time(&self.schema, &tx, now)?;
        let count = seed_rows(&self.schema, &tx, rows, now)?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(count)
    }

    /// Delete a row as the system principal: constraints, aggregates and
    /// partition events apply. Returns the number of rows removed (0 or 1).
    pub fn seed_delete(&mut self, table: &str, id: &str) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.schema.refuse_ephemeral_import(table)?;
        timeline::check_import(&self.schema, table)?;
        let now = self.advance_time_audience(crate::now_ms())?;
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let now = time_audience::pin_writer_time(&self.schema, &tx, now)?;
        let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
        let mut runner = Runner::new(&tx, &self.schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(if crate::schema::live::has_live(&self.schema) {Ceilings::job()}else{Ceilings::unbounded()}, Cut::Abort), seq);
        if crate::schema::live::has_live(&self.schema) {runner.reserve_bulk_source(table)?;}
        let removed = match runner.delete(table, id) {
            Ok(()) => 1,
            Err(RuntimeError::Require { .. }) => 0,
            Err(error) => return Err(error),
        };
        let events = runner.events.take();
        let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
        drop(runner);
        if removed > 0 {
            tx.record_commit(seq, now, "seed", crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, "seed", &tables, removed)?;
            partition::append(&tx, seq, &events)?;
        }
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(removed)
    }

    /// Untrusted fixture import. All targets are installed before attribution
    /// is captured, so import order cannot invent dangling references.
    pub fn load(&mut self, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.load_mode(rows, LoadMode::Import)
    }
// @ref LLP 3000.033#1.6 — the device has no Engine; this option is server-only.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum DeliveryManifest { Build, Skip }

/// `/seed`'s write inside the caller's transaction: one commit when any row is written.
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
pub(crate) fn seed_rows(schema: &Schema, tx: &crate::store::Tx<'_>, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>, now: i64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    let bounded=crate::schema::live::has_live(schema);
    let mut bulk_meter=Meter::new(if bounded {Ceilings::job()}else{Ceilings::unbounded()},Cut::Abort);
    let rows:Box<dyn Iterator<Item=(String,Value)> + '_>=if bounded {Box::new(live_bulk::collect(rows,&mut bulk_meter)?.into_iter())}else{Box::new(rows.into_iter())};
    let rows = through_activation::ancestor_order(schema, rows);
    let targets: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = schema.tables.values().flat_map(|table| table.recorded.values().map(|field| field.table.as_str())).collect();
    let mut imports = BTreeMap::<(String, String), Value>::new();
    let rows: Box<dyn Iterator<Item = (String, Value)> + '_> = if targets.is_empty() { Box::new(rows.into_iter()) } else {
        let rows: Vec<_> = rows.into_iter().collect();
        // A capture sees the imported target's eventual image, including
        // fields retained by patches, defaults and assigned ordinals.
        // Preview every allocation in input order (counters cross tables),
        // then undo the preview; ordinary writes allocate in that same
        // transaction and still own all validation, events and rollback.
        tx.savepoint("seed_sequences")?;
        let mut staging = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), bulk_meter, 0);
        for (name, row) in &rows {
            if bounded {staging.reserve_bulk_source(name)?;staging.meter.heap(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(&schema.tables[name]).saturating_mul(4))?;}
            schema.refuse_ephemeral_import(name)?;
            let table = schema.table(name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {name}")))?;
            sequences::refuse_fields(table, name, row)?;
            let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
            let old = match imports.get(&(name.clone(), id.clone())) { Some(row) => Some(row.clone()), None => tx.get(name, &id)? }.filter(|row| !table.expired(row, now));
            let mut fields = old.as_ref().map(|row| row.as_object().cloned()).transpose()?.unwrap_or_default();
            fields.extend(row.as_object()?.clone());
            if old.is_none() { for (field, default) in &table.defaults { fields.entry(field.clone()).or_insert_with(|| default.value(now, crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL)); } }
            let mut image = Value::Row(fields.into());
            if old.is_none() {
                let (assigned, full) = staging.sequence_fields(name, image.import_input(&ImportInput(())))?;
                if full { return Err(sequences::exhausted()); }
                staging.commit_sequences(name, &assigned)?;
                image = assigned;
            }
            imports.insert((name.clone(), id), image);
        }
        bulk_meter=staging.meter.clone();
        drop(staging);
        tx.rollback_to("seed_sequences")?;
        Box::new(rows.into_iter())
    };
    let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
    let mut runner = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), bulk_meter, seq);
    runner.import_targets = Some(&imports);
    let mut count = 0;
    for (table, row) in rows {
        if bounded {runner.reserve_bulk_source(&table)?;}
        schema.refuse_ephemeral_import(&table)?;
        timeline::check_import(schema, &table)?;
        runner.upsert(&table, &row.field("id")?.clone(), row.import_input(&ImportInput(())))?;
        count += 1;
    }
    // Seeds preserve ordinary expired images for a later sweep. A source of
    // a row-keyed view is the established exception: an already-expired
    // seeded image must not enter a committed count basis or revive after
    // a clock rollback. Route only those sources through the common retirement
    // path so reference actions and net maintenance still run transactionally.
    // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.5 — seeded expired row-view sources retire with actions; unrelated expired fixtures remain stored.
    let final_exists = runner.satisfied_final_exists()?;
    runner.retire_seeded_view_sources()?;
    // Seeds historically admit fixture rows whose obligations are not
    // meaningful under the system viewer. Retirement may not invalidate an
    // obligation that did hold immediately before its reference actions.
    runner.check_final_exists_witnesses(&final_exists)?;
    let events = runner.events.take();
    let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
    drop(runner);
    if count > 0 {
        tx.record_commit(seq, now, "seed", crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, "seed", &tables, count)?;
        partition::append(tx, seq, &events)?;
    }
//! Advice identifies a risk shape; it never supplies a proof assumption.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#6-bakeoff-repairs — current roles, grant writers, required evidence and refusal oracles.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramKind};
fn rule_parts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(rule_parts).collect(),RuleExpr::Not(x)=>rule_parts(x),r=>vec![r]}}
fn role_test(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {rule_parts(rule).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..} if key.iter().any(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&equalities.iter().any(|(_,t)|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Literal(_)))))}
fn reader_columns(rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{rule_parts(rule).into_iter().filter_map(|r|match r{RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))=>Some(f.clone()),_=>None}).collect()}
fn note(notes:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,rewrite:&str){notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,rewrite).guide("security"));}
fn mutable_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool {
    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(name)else{return false;};
    let roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
    if roles.is_empty(){return false;}
    let mutable=roles.iter().any(|c|!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
    mutable&&bound.programs.iter().any(|p|snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==name&&matches!(*kind,"update"|"upsert"|"delete")))
}
pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        if t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable(){note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by every signed-in user: {}",t.rules.read.quarry()),"confirm the spec permits these columns and declare read intent; snapback4 audit lists inherited grants and their writers");}
        if let RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster,key,equalities,..}=&t.rules.read
            && equalities.is_empty()&&key.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer)) {
            note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by any signed-in user with a matching {roster} row; no particular role or row ownership is required"),"confirm that every roster member should see all these columns; declare intent from the privacy spec and inspect audit");
        }
        for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            if kind.contains_asset() && (kind.is_optional() || matches!(kind.required(),C::List{..}|C::ListRange{min:0,..})) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE",format!("{name}.{column} permits omitted or empty media evidence"),"if the spec requires evidence, declare a required image/file or [image] 1..N; otherwise retain optionality deliberately (guide security)");
            }
        }
        for leaf in rule_parts(&t.rules.insert) {
            if let RuleExpr::Created {table:parent,..}=leaf
                && bound.schema.table(parent).is_some_and(|p| p.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().any(|r| matches!(r,RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(_)))))
                && bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other| [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r| rule_parts(r).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&matches!(key.as_slice(),[RuleTerm::Viewer])&&equalities.is_empty())))) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_VACUOUS_RULE",format!("{name}: `created {parent}` can bootstrap authority trusted globally by a viewer-only probe; a caller may create their own global administrator grant"),"provision global authority only through an operator/service seat or existing authorized admin; group creation must scope every later grant to that group (guide deploy, first-admin setup)");
            }
        }
        for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)] {
          for branch in rule.disjuncts(){
            if role_test(branch){continue;}
            for dep in kernel::dependencies(branch){if let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep) {
                let rosters=[&g.rules.insert,&g.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).filter(|r|mutable_role(bound,r)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
                if !rosters.is_empty()&&(role_test(&g.rules.insert)||role_test(&g.rules.update)){
                    note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: `{}` trusts retained {dep} grants while current role in {} can change",branch.quarry(),rosters.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),"recheck the current role in this branch or revoke every derived grant transactionally on role changes; guide security");
                }
            }}
            if !reader_columns(branch).is_empty()&&[&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).any(|r|mutable_role(bound,&r))&&(role_test(&t.rules.insert)||role_test(&t.rules.update)) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: stored principal `{}` grants authority after a mutable role changes",branch.quarry()),"recheck the current role as well as stored attribution; guide security");
            }
          }
        }
    }
    // Inspect original syntax for argument flow and distinct authored codes.
    // Keeping lints outside Bound.notes preserves the compiler's existing
    // diagnostic contract; check/audit expose this security advice separately.
    let _=exposures;
    notes
}
pub(super) fn source_notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for (file,source) in sources{
        let (doc,_)=crate::parse_document(file,source);
        for p in &doc.programs {
            let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
            let mut guarded=BTreeSet::new();
            for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body) {
                if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=statement {
                    let mut condition=condition.clone();
                    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition,&mut |e|if let Expr::Exists{read,..}=e {
                        for key in &read.keys {if let Expr::Name{name,..}=&key.value {guarded.insert(name.clone());}}
                    });
                }
            }
            super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e|{
                let Expr::Write{table,fields,site,..}=e else{return};
                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{return};
                let role_columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
                for column in &role_columns {if fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)&&bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other|[&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(table))) {
                    let targets=fields.iter().filter(|f|&f.name==column).filter_map(|f|match &f.value{Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(s),..})=>Some(s.clone()),_=>None}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                    if let Some(risk)=super::privilege::risk(bound,table,column,&targets)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),"$privilege".into())) {
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION",format!("{}: {risk}; review the grant's delegation policy",p.name),"restrict role assignment to authorized delegation or an operator seat; use guide staging's first-admin recipe (intentional open enrollment may permit this)");
                    }
                }}
                let readers=reader_columns(&t.rules.read);
                for f in fields.iter(){
                    let arg=f.value.as_ref().and_then(|e|if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e{Some(name.as_str())}else{None}).unwrap_or(f.name.as_str());
                    if !guarded.contains(arg)&&readers.contains(&f.name)&&p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==arg)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),f.name.clone())){
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT",format!("{} writes argument {arg} straight into reader-grant column {table}.{}",p.name,f.name),"require the chosen principal's current role and relationship before granting access; caller authority alone does not validate the recipient");
                    }

                }
            });
            // Distinct absent and denial codes disclose logical existence to
            // callers admitted by a broader table rule, even without payload.
            fn refusals(body:&[Stmt],bound:&Bound,absent:&mut Option<String>,codes:&mut Vec<(Site,String)>){for s in body{match s{
                Stmt::Bind{value:Expr::Assert{value,code,..},..}=>{
                    *absent=None;
                    if let Expr::Read(read)=value.as_ref()&&let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&read.table){
                        let own=read.keys.iter().any(|k|matches!(&k.value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"));
                        if !t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()&&!own {
                            // A table read gate already conflates hidden and absent.
                            // Only broader program-level privacy intent can make
                            // its later refusal distinguish private existence.
                            if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==read.table&&i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&t.rules.read,policy)==Ok(false))){*absent=Some(code.clone());}
                        }
                    }
                },
                Stmt::Require{site,code,..}=>if let Some(a)=absent.as_ref()&&a!=code{codes.push((site.clone(),format!("lookup absence {a}, later denial {code}")));},
                Stmt::If{then_body,else_body,..}=>{refusals(then_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);refusals(else_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);},
                Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>refusals(body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes),_=>{}}}}
            let mut codes=Vec::new();refusals(&p.body,bound,&mut None,&mut codes);
            for (site,why) in codes.into_iter().take(1){note(&mut out,&site,"N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE",format!("{}: {why}; row readership is broader than declared private-existence intent",p.name),"use the same absence/denial code and message for private existence, or tighten the row audience");}
            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Job {
                fn guards(body:&[Stmt],out:&mut Vec<Expr>){for s in body {match s{
                    Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>out.push(condition.clone()),
                    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{out.push(condition.clone());guards(then_body,out);guards(else_body,out);},
                    Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>guards(body,out),_=>{}
                }}}
                let mut job_guards=Vec::new();guards(&p.body,&mut job_guards);
                let clock=job_guards.iter().any(|g|{let mut g=g.clone();let mut found=false;super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut g,&mut |e|if matches!(e,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"){found=true;});found});
                if !clock{continue;}
                let mut writes=BTreeSet::new();let mut b=p.body.clone();
                super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut b,&mut |e|if let Expr::Write{table,fields,..}=e&&fields.iter().any(|f|f.name=="status"){writes.insert(table.clone());});
                for table in writes {for m in doc.programs.iter().filter(|m|m.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation){
                    let mut aliases=BTreeSet::new();
                    for s in &m.body{if let Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=s{
                        let v=if let Expr::Assert{value,..}=value{value.as_ref()}else{value};
                        if matches!(v,Expr::Read(r) if r.table==table){aliases.extend(names.clone());}
                    }}
                    let mut checks=Vec::new();guards(&m.body,&mut checks);let(mut status,mut now)=(false,false);
                    for mut c in checks{super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut c,&mut |e|match e{
                        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"=>now=true,
                        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="status"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if aliases.contains(name))=>status=true,_=>{}
                    });}
                    let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
                    let job_name = if module == "schema" { p.name.clone() } else { format!("{module}.{}", p.name) };
                    let already_named = bound.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" && n.site == m.site
                        && n.message.contains(&format!("column {table}.status)"))
                        && n.message.contains(&format!("scheduled job {job_name} ")));
                    if status&&!now&&!already_named{note(&mut out,&m.site,"N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE",format!("{} gates on {table}.status while clock job {} changes that status; writes can run between the deadline and sweep",m.name,p.name),"enforce the deadline against server now in the mutation too; a sweep cleans up state but does not enforce an exact deadline");}
                }}
            }
        }
    }
    out
}

    58	pub(super) fn source_notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    59	    let mut out=Vec::new();
    60	    for (file,source) in sources{
    61	        let (doc,_)=crate::parse_document(file,source);
    62	        for p in &doc.programs {
    63	            let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
    64	            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
    65	            let mut guarded=BTreeSet::new();
    66	            for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body) {
    67	                if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=statement {
    68	                    let mut condition=condition.clone();
    69	                    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition,&mut |e|if let Expr::Exists{read,..}=e {
    70	                        for key in &read.keys {if let Expr::Name{name,..}=&key.value {guarded.insert(name.clone());}}
    71	                    });
    72	                }
    73	            }
    74	            super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e|{
    75	                let Expr::Write{table,fields,site,..}=e else{return};
    76	                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{return};
    77	                let role_columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
    78	                for column in &role_columns {if fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)&&bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other|[&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(table))) {
    79	                    let targets=fields.iter().filter(|f|&f.name==column).filter_map(|f|match &f.value{Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(s),..})=>Some(s.clone()),_=>None}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    80	                    if let Some(risk)=super::privilege::risk(bound,table,column,&targets)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),"$privilege".into())) {
    81	                        note(&mut out,site,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION",format!("{}: {risk}; review the grant's delegation policy",p.name),"restrict role assignment to authorized delegation or an operator seat; use guide staging's first-admin recipe (intentional open enrollment may permit this)");
    82	                    }
    83	                }}
    84	                let readers=reader_columns(&t.rules.read);
    85	                for f in fields.iter(){
    86	                    let arg=f.value.as_ref().and_then(|e|if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e{Some(name.as_str())}else{None}).unwrap_or(f.name.as_str());
    87	                    if !guarded.contains(arg)&&readers.contains(&f.name)&&p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==arg)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),f.name.clone())){
    88	                        note(&mut out,site,"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT",format!("{} writes argument {arg} straight into reader-grant column {table}.{}",p.name,f.name),"require the chosen principal's current role and relationship before granting access; caller authority alone does not validate the recipient");
    89	                    }
    90	
    91	                }
    92	            });
    93	            // Distinct absent and denial codes disclose logical existence to
    94	            // callers admitted by a broader table rule, even without payload.
    95	            fn refusals(body:&[Stmt],bound:&Bound,absent:&mut Option<String>,codes:&mut Vec<(Site,String)>){for s in body{match s{
    96	                Stmt::Bind{value:Expr::Assert{value,code,..},..}=>{
    97	                    *absent=None;
    98	                    if let Expr::Read(read)=value.as_ref()&&let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&read.table){
    99	                        let own=read.keys.iter().any(|k|matches!(&k.value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"));
   100	                        if !t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()&&!own {
   101	                            // A table read gate already conflates hidden and absent.
   102	                            // Only broader program-level privacy intent can make
   103	                            // its later refusal distinguish private existence.
   104	                            if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==read.table&&i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&t.rules.read,policy)==Ok(false))){*absent=Some(code.clone());}
   105	                        }

#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
impl Engine {
    pub fn new(store: Store, schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_at(store, schema, programs, crate::now_ms())
    }

    /// Activate using the owning host's clock for parent liveness and captures.
    pub fn new_at(mut store: Store, mut schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>, now: i64) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        columns::validate_before_prepare(&schema)?;
        schema.check_row_view_audiences()?;
        schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
        schema.prepare_delivery_lookup();
        let issues = crate::schema::validate(&schema);
        if let Some(issue) = issues.first() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{}: {}", issue.path, issue.message)));
        }
        let programs = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
        let (generation, metadata) = Self::activate(&mut store, &schema, &programs, None, now, true, None, None)?;
        let dev_effects = metadata.dev_effects;
        store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        Ok(Self { timeline_schedule: timeline::Schedule::default(), ephemeral: std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(metadata.store_id, generation, &schema)), store, schema, programs, generation, expiry_after: BTreeMap::new(), dev_effects, ceilings: Ceilings::default(), entropy: crate::ids::seed(), minter: crate::ids::Mint::default(), #[cfg(feature = "native")] native: None, #[cfg(feature = "native")] native_logs: native::Logs::default(), presence: None })
    }

    /// A detached read owner for HTTP query/sync computation. Creating it is
    /// brief; materializing its response does not hold the serving engine.
    pub fn reader(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Ok(Self { timeline_schedule: timeline::Schedule::default(), ephemeral: self.ephemeral.clone(), store: self.store.reader()?, schema: self.schema.clone(), programs: self.programs.clone(),
            ceilings: self.ceilings, entropy: 0, minter: self.minter.clone(), generation: self.generation, expiry_after: BTreeMap::new(), dev_effects: self.dev_effects,
            #[cfg(feature = "native")] native: self.native.clone(),
            #[cfg(feature = "native")] native_logs: native::Logs::default(), presence: self.presence.clone() })
    }

    /// The registry every program's `connected[p]` reads, on this engine.
    pub fn set_presence(&mut self, presence: Option<std::sync::Arc<dyn Presence>>) {
        self.presence = presence;
    }


    /// Attach to an already activated semantic generation without installing
    /// caller source. Reads of its identity and compiled content are one snapshot.
    pub fn open(store: Store) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::open_at(store, crate::now_ms())
    }

    pub fn open_at(store: Store, now: i64) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::open_selected_at(store, now, None)
    }

    fn open_selected_at(mut store: Store, now: i64, expected_release: Option<u64>) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = store.opening_transaction()?;
        Store::check_selected_release(tx.conn(), expected_release)?;
        let (generation, schema, programs): (i64, String, String) = tx.conn().query_row(
            "SELECT g.generation, g.schema, g.programs FROM _generations g JOIN _meta m ON m.key = 'generation' AND g.generation = CAST(m.value AS INTEGER)",
            [], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?, row.get(2)?)))
            .map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(format!("no readable activated backend in this store: {e}")))?;
        let kept = crate::store::opening::Backend(generation, schema.clone(), programs.clone());
        let generation = u64::try_from(generation).map_err(|_| RuntimeError::Store("negative stored generation".into()))?;
        let schema = serde_json::from_str(&schema).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e, |e| RuntimeError::Store(format!("invalid stored schema: {e}"))))?;
        crate::schema::timeline::check_version(&schema)?;
        timeline::check_no_rebuilds(&tx, &schema)?;

/// Every table a program writes, by statement kind.
pub fn write_tables(program: &Program) -> Vec<(String, &'static str)> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    stmts_write_tables(&program.body, &mut out);
    out
}

//! Typed server-clock facts used only by the proof abstraction, never runtime rules.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — lifetime is independent of physical sweep lag.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Value, ir::BinaryOp as Op, schema::{Table, ColumnType}};
// Quarry identifiers cannot contain ':'. No authored field aliases this atom.
const CLOCK: &str = "$security:server-clock";
pub(super) fn clock() -> RuleTerm { RuleTerm::Field(CLOCK.into()) }
pub(super) fn has_clock(rule: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
    rule.any_term(&|t| matches!(t, RuleTerm::Field(f) if f == CLOCK))
}
pub(super) fn comparison(op: Op, a: RuleTerm, b: RuleTerm) -> RuleExpr {
    match op {
        Op::Gt => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lt,b,a), Op::Gte => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lte,b,a),
        Op::Eq => RuleExpr::Eq(a,b), Op::Ne => RuleExpr::Ne(a,b),
        _ => RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b),
    }
}
pub(super) fn admission(table: &Table) -> RuleExpr {
    let mut facts = vec![table.rules.read.clone()];
    for (column,op) in table.not_before.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Lte)).chain(table.expire.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Gt))) {
        let field=RuleTerm::Field(column.clone());
        let edge=comparison(op,field.clone(),clock());
        let edge=if table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(ColumnType::is_optional) {
            RuleExpr::Or(vec![RuleExpr::Eq(field,RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),edge])
        } else {edge};
        facts.push(edge);
    }
    RuleExpr::And(facts)
}

pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, info: &super::super::catalog::TableInfo, expr: &ast::Expr) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    use ast::{Expr,Literal,TypeKind};
    fn term(info:&super::super::catalog::TableInfo,e:&Expr)->Option<RuleTerm>{match e {
        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now" => Some(clock()),
        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..}) && info.columns.get(name).is_some_and(|t|t.kind==TypeKind::Time) => Some(RuleTerm::Field(name.clone())),
        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Number(n),..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(n.parse().ok()?))),
        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Null,..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),
        _=>None,
    }}
    if let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=expr {
        if op=="and"||op=="or" {
            let xs=vec![bind(catalog,info,left)?,bind(catalog,info,right)?];
            return Some(if op=="and"{RuleExpr::And(xs)}else{RuleExpr::Or(xs)});
        }
        let op=match op.as_str(){"="=>Op::Eq,"!="=>Op::Ne,"<"=>Op::Lt,"<="=>Op::Lte,">"=>Op::Gt,">="=>Op::Gte,_=>return None};
        if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(term(info,left),term(info,right)){return Some(comparison(op,a,b));}
    }
    if let Expr::Unary{op,value,..}=expr && op=="not" {return Some(RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(bind(catalog,info,value)?)));}
    let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:expr.clone(),site:expr.site().clone()};
    super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule).ok()
}

snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/engine/sequences.rs:361:    fn exists(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value], equalities: &[(String, Value)]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
//! Expanded helpers can write from any expression block.
// @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — legacy eligibility examines the complete expanded write footprint.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn stmts_write_tables(stmts: &[Stmt], out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    for stmt in stmts {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Insert { table, .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "insert")),
            Stmt::Update { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "update")),
            Stmt::Upsert { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "upsert")),
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, upsert, .. } => out.push((target.table.clone(), if *upsert { "upsert" } else { "update" })),
            Stmt::Delete { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "delete")),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => out.push((target.table.clone(), "delete")),
            _ => {}
        }
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr_write_tables(value, out),
            Stmt::Emit { payload: value, once, .. } => { expr_write_tables(value, out); if let Some(value) = once { expr_write_tables(value, out); } },
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                stmts_write_tables(then_body, out);
                stmts_write_tables(else_body, out);
            }
            Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
                match domain {
                    LoopDomain::Page(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
                    LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => { expr_write_tables(start, out); expr_write_tables(end_exclusive, out); }
                    LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
                    LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse) => {
                        expr_write_tables(&recurse.seed, out);
                    }
                    LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => {}
                }
                stmts_write_tables(body, out);
            }
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expr_write_tables(condition, out),
            Stmt::Check { condition, current } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                for key in &current.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { row, .. } | Stmt::Insert { row, .. } => expr_write_tables(row, out),
            Stmt::Update { key, patch, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
            }
            Stmt::Upsert { key, row, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(row, out);
            }
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, condition, .. } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
                if let Some(condition) = condition { stmts_write_tables(&condition.setup, out); expr_write_tables(&condition.value, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Delete { key, .. } => expr_write_tables(key, out),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => {}
        }
    }
}

fn scan_write_tables(scan: &Scan, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    if let Some(filter) = &scan.filter { expr_write_tables(filter, out); }
    if let Some(range) = &scan.range {
        for bound in [&range.gt, &range.gte, &range.lt, &range.lte].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(bound, out); }
    }
    for key in &scan.prefix {
        match key { IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(expr) | IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(expr) => expr_write_tables(expr, out) }
    }
    for cursor in [&scan.after, &scan.before].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(cursor, out); }
}

fn expr_write_tables(expr: &Expr, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    match expr {
        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) | Expr::Input(_) => {}
        Expr::Field { base, .. } => expr_write_tables(base, out),
        Expr::Array(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => { expr_write_tables(base, out); fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)); }
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            expr_write_tables(left, out);
            expr_write_tables(right, out);
        }
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
        Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Get(read) | Expr::Exists(read) => {
            read.key.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out));
        }
        Expr::Block { body, value } => { stmts_write_tables(body, out); expr_write_tables(value, out); }
        Expr::Scan(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            if let Some(after) = merge.position.as_ref().and_then(|position| position.after.as_ref()) { expr_write_tables(after, out); }
            for lane in &merge.lanes {
                if let Some(guard) = &lane.guard { expr_write_tables(guard, out); }
                if let Some(project) = &lane.project { expr_write_tables(project, out); }
                if let Some(value) = &lane.distinct { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                stmts_write_tables(&lane.setup, out);
                if let Some(source) = &lane.source {
                    if let Some(scan) = source.scan() { scan_write_tables(scan, out); }
                    for value in source.expressions() { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                }
                scan_write_tables(&lane.scan, out);
            }
        }
    }
}

    85	fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    86	    for inv in &bound.invariants{
    87	        if inv.target=="read"||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
    88	        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
    89	        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    90	        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
    91	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
    92	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
    93	            if old==Some(row){continue;}
    94	            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
    95	            if inv.target!="always"&&inv.operation.is_none()&&!super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
    96	            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
    97	            if inv.target=="always" {
    98	                if policy(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}

   100	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();

   102	                if policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old)==Ok(false)){
   103	                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
   104	                }
   105	            }
   106	        }
   107	    }

   109	pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   110	    if bound.invariants.is_empty(){return;}
   111	    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   112	    if initial.values().all(Vec::is_empty){out.unsupported.insert("history needs a trusted fixture; generated snapshots have separate reachability labels".into());return;}
   113	    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
   114	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   115	    let probes=Vec::new();
   116	    let mut groups=Vec::new();
   117	    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
   118	        let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,&initial,&actors,out);
   119	        let mut group=Vec::new();
   120	        for args in cases{for viewer in &actors{group.push((&bp.program,viewer.clone(),args.clone()));}}
   121	        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
   122	    }
   123	    let mut candidates=Vec::new();
   124	    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){candidates.push(c.clone());}}}
   125	    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   126	    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
   127	    // Breadth first, then diagonal pairs: no first step monopolizes the cap.
   128	    let singles=candidates.len().min(LIMIT);
   129	    for n in 0..singles+LIMIT {
   130	        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let k=n-singles;vec![k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()]};
   131	        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   132	        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
   133	        for i in indices{
   134	            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   135	            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   136	            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
   137	                Ok(result)=>{
   138	                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
   139	                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
   140	                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   141	                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
   142	                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);}
   143	                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
   144	            }
   145	        }
   146	    }
   147	    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
   148	}
    20	}
    21	fn run_pair(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,out:&mut Campaign){
    22	    let Some(other)=sample_rows(bound,rows,inv,row)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column pair needs a second typed non-asset value",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or("")));return;};
    23	    let altrow=other[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
    24	    let mut actors=histories::viewers(bound);
    25	    for (table,rs) in rows{for r in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=r.field(c)&&!actors.contains(p){actors.push(p.clone());}}}}
    26	    for viewer in actors{
    27	        if policy(bound,inv,rows,&viewer,None,row)!=Ok(false)||policy(bound,inv,&other,&viewer,None,altrow)!=Ok(false){continue;}
    28	        for bp in &bound.programs{
    29	            let p=&bp.program;
    30	            if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native{continue;}
    31	            let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
    32	            if json.contains("NewId")||json.contains("Emit"){continue;}
    33	            let mut args_list=histories::argument_cases(&p.args,out);
    34	            for args in &mut args_list{for (name,kind) in &p.args{if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(r)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())&&let Ok(id)=r.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}}}
    35	            for args in args_list{
    36	                let execute=|alternate:bool|->Result<serde_json::Value,String>{
    37	                    let seeds=if alternate{&other}else{rows};
    38	                    let mut e=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{
    39	                        let mut e=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())?;
    40	                        if alternate{
    41	                            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
    42	                            let Value::Row(fields)=altrow else{return Err("not a row".into());};
    43	                            let clean=Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect());
    44	                            e.seed_at([(inv.table.clone(),clean)],NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    45	                        }e
    46	                    }else{engine(bound,seeds,program("$column.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))?};
    47	                    let actual=readback(&e)?;
    48	                    let current=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).ok_or("column target is absent after validation")?;
    49	                    if policy(bound,inv,&actual,&viewer,None,current)!=Ok(false){return Err("validated column-world policy is not false".into());}
    50	                    if alternate&&fixture.is_some(){
    51	                        for (table,rs) in rows.iter().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()){
    52	                            let definition=&bound.schema.tables[table];
    53	                            let same=rs.iter().all(|old|actual.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|definition.columns.keys().filter(|c|!definition.maintained.contains_key(*c)&&!(table==&inv.table&&inv.column.as_ref()==Some(*c)&&old.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())).all(|c|old.field(c).ok()==new.field(c).ok())));
    54	                            if !same{return Err(format!("alternate seed changes non-protected base fields in {table}; capture/recorded reduction unavailable"));}
    55	                        }
    56	                    }
    57	                    paired::observe(&mut e,p,&viewer,args.clone())
    58	                };
    59	                let (a,b)=match (execute(false),execute(true)){
    60	                    (Ok(a),Ok(b))=>(a,b),
    61	                    (Err(why),_)|(_,Err(why))=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{} / {}: declined column world: {why}",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),p.name));continue;}
    62	                };
    63	                out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
    64	                if a==b{continue;}
    65	                if !matches!((execute(false),execute(true)),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if aa==a&&bb==b){continue;}
    66	                out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:if fixture.is_some(){Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace}else{Reachability::SyntheticOperation},invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer,operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"column read observation".into(),row:row.clone(),old:Some(altrow.clone()),supporting_rows:rows.clone(),history:Vec::new(),rule_path:format!("authored observer depends on {}.{} while policy is false in both typed worlds",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()),scope:if fixture.is_some(){"primary observation executes on trusted fixture; alternate column is a dependency test, not an authored alternate-history claim"}else{"schema-valid synthetic worlds; authored observer, no application reachability claim"}.into(),observations:Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:other.clone()})});
    67	                return;
    68	            }
    69	        }
    70	    }
    71	}
    72	pub(super) fn generated(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    73	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_some()&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))){
    74	        for variant in 0..16{
    75	            let rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column snapshot: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
    76	            let e=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$column.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column seed: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
    77	            let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
    78	            for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,None,out);}
    79	            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){break;}
    80	        }
    81	    }
    82	}
    83	pub(super) fn fixture(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    84	    let Ok(rows)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    85	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_some()){
    86	        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),out);}
    87	    }

// One cached probe answer avoids refetching an unchanged fact in a loop.
// Writes invalidate it; cache hits still pay the declared logical probe charge.
#[derive(PartialEq)]
pub(super) struct WitnessKey {
    revision: u64, table: String, index: String, key: Vec<Value>,
    equalities: Vec<(String, Value)>, current: bool,
}

pub(super) struct RuleProbe<'a, 'b> {
    pub(super) runner: &'a mut Runner<'b>,
    pub(super) allowance: &'a Authorization,
}
impl Probe for RuleProbe<'_, '_> {
    fn reference(&mut self, table: &str, id: &Value, field: &str) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> { self.runner.rule_reference(table, id, field) }
    fn take_changes(&mut self) -> Option<Vec<(i64, super::rules::Verdict)>> { self.runner.witness_changes.take() }
    fn replace_expiry(&mut self, expiry: Option<i64>) -> Option<i64> { self.runner.next_expiry.replace(expiry) }
    fn exists(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value], equalities: &[(String, Value)]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.runner.rule_probe_exists(self.allowance, table, index, key, equalities)
    }
    fn created(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value]) -> bool {
        self.runner.created(table, index, key)
    }
}

/// The scanned prefix as the row a rule is judged against: each pinned column with its value.
fn prefix_row(index: &Index, prefix: &[Value]) -> Value {
    Value::Row(index.components.iter().zip(prefix).filter_map(|(part, value)| part.column().map(|name| (name.clone(), value.clone()))).collect::<std::collections::BTreeMap<_, _>>().into())
}

impl<'r> Runner<'r> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — the dependency envelope is schema-derived too; short-circuit probes cannot disclose a hidden answer through tables.
    pub(super) fn rule_dependencies(&mut self, rule: &crate::schema::RuleExpr) {
        let mut sites = Vec::new();
        rules::probe_sites(rule, &mut sites);
        for (table, _) in sites {
            if let Some(key) = self.schema.maintain_for(&table).and_then(|m|m.row_key.as_ref()) {
                self.read_tables.insert(key.table.clone());
            }
            self.aggregate_source_dependency(&table);
            self.read_tables.insert(table);
        }
    }

    fn metered_aggregate_source_dependency(&mut self, table: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if let Some(maintain) = self.schema.maintain_for(table)
            .filter(|maintain| self.schema.tables[&maintain.source].is_expiring()) {
            metered_dependency_table(&mut self.meter, &mut self.read_tables, &maintain.source)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
            let covered = match audience {
                Audience::Public => true,
                Audience::Column(column) => pinned.get(column).is_some_and(|value| value.as_str() == Ok(self.context.viewer.as_str())),
                Audience::Target { table: target, prefix, .. } => {
                    let Some(mut group) = prefix.iter().map(|column| pinned.get(column).map(|value| (*value).clone())).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>() else { continue };
                    let binding = crate::schema::audience_binding(self.schema, table, definition, sync).map_err(RuntimeError::Schema)?;
                    group.push(Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone()));
                    let target_def = &self.schema.tables[target];
                    self.read_tables.insert(target.clone());
                    // This is a raw audience membership, not the witness's ACL.
                    let found = self.live_lookup(allowance, target, &binding.group_index, target_def.index(&binding.group_index).expect("audience index"), &group, #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] None)?;
                    found.map(|row| self.witness_covered(target, &binding.group_index, &group, &row)).transpose()?.unwrap_or(false)
                }
            };
            if covered { return Ok(true); }
        }
        Ok(false)
    }
}

struct ReadProbe<'a, 'b> { runner: &'a mut Runner<'b>, allowance: &'a Authorization, table: &'a str, provisional: bool, prepaid: bool, unknown: bool }
impl Probe for ReadProbe<'_, '_> {
    fn take_unknown(&mut self) -> bool { std::mem::take(&mut self.unknown) }
    fn take_changes(&mut self) -> Option<Vec<(i64, super::rules::Verdict)>> { self.runner.witness_changes.take() }
    fn replace_expiry(&mut self, expiry: Option<i64>) -> Option<i64> { self.runner.next_expiry.replace(expiry) }
    fn field(&mut self, field: &str, _old: bool) {
        self.runner.provisional_sequence_read |= self.provisional && self.runner.schema.tables[self.table].sequences.contains_key(field);
    }
    fn exists(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value], equalities: &[(String, Value)]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if !self.prepaid && !self.runner.read_charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, 1)? { return Ok(false); }
        let (present, known) = self.runner.probe_witness(self.allowance, table, index, key, equalities, true)?;
        self.unknown |= !known;
        Ok(present)
    }
    fn created(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value]) -> bool { self.runner.created(table, index, key) }
}

mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::{Context, meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter}, rows::{MemoryRows, Rows}, value::object};

    #[test]
    fn provisional_authorization_scopes_expiry_to_succeeded_predicates() {
        let witness = "exists grants[parent = .parent, who = viewer, kind = 'admin']";
        for kind in ["guest", "admin"] {
            for rule in [format!("{witness} or .owner = viewer"), format!("({witness} and .owner != viewer) or .owner = viewer"), witness.into()] {
                let source = format!("table grants:\n  parent: text <=10\n  who: principal\n  kind: text <=10\n  until: time ?\n  expire at .until\n  unique parent, who\n  public\n  sync public\ntable notes:\n  parent: text <=10\n  owner: principal\n  read <- {rule}\n  online only\n");
                let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
                assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
                let schema: crate::schema::Schema = crate::json_codec::from_text(&crate::json_codec::to_text(&bound.schema).unwrap()).unwrap();
                for until in [2000, 3000] {
                    let rows = MemoryRows::default();
                    let grant = object([("id", Value::Id("g".into())), ("parent", Value::String("p".into())), ("who", Value::Id("alice".into())), ("kind", Value::String(kind.into())), ("until", Value::Int(until))]);
                    rows.put("grants", &schema.tables["grants"], &grant, None).unwrap();
                    let candidate = object([("id", Value::Id("own".into())), ("parent", Value::String("p".into())), ("owner", Value::Id("alice".into()))]);
                    let mut runner = Runner::new(&rows, &schema, Context::new("alice", Default::default()).at(1000), Meter::new(Ceilings::default(), Cut::Cap), 0);
                    runner.device = true;
                    runner.provisional_rows.insert(("notes".into(), "own".into()));
                    runner.next_expiry = crate::engine::expiry::Expiry::from(Some(9000)); // A separate earlier read's dependency survives.
                    let scope = runner.next_expiry.scope(); // Deadlines are provisional until a read emits them.
                    assert_eq!(runner.authorize_read("notes", &candidate, false).unwrap(), rule != witness || kind == "admin");
                    scope.emit();
                    assert_eq!(runner.next_expiry.get(), Some(if rule == witness && kind == "admin" { until } else { 9000 }), "{kind}/{rule}");
                }
            }
        }
    }

//! Field writes use the old owner; row transition rules still constrain them.
// @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — ownership grants only the explicitly touched owned fields.
use super::runner::Runner;
use super::rules::{self, Probe};
use crate::{error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError}, schema::{Effect, RuleExpr}, value::Value};

/// Delivery accounting must not make another recipient's unknown membership
/// a dependency of the writer's prediction. Server witnesses remain exact.
struct DeliveryProbe<'a, 'b> {
    runner: &'a mut Runner<'b>,
    known: bool,
    allowance: &'a super::Authorization,
}

impl Probe for DeliveryProbe<'_, '_> {
    fn take_changes(&mut self) -> Option<Vec<(i64, super::rules::Verdict)>> { self.runner.witness_changes.take() }
    fn replace_expiry(&mut self, expiry: Option<i64>) -> Option<i64> { self.runner.next_expiry.replace(expiry) }
    fn exists(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value], equalities: &[(String, Value)]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if !self.runner.read_charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, 1)? { return Ok(false); }
        let (present, known) = self.runner.probe_witness(self.allowance, table, index, key, equalities, false)?;
        self.known &= known;
        Ok(present)
    }

    fn created(&mut self, _table: &str, _index: &str, _key: &[Value]) -> bool { false }
}

impl<'r> Runner<'r> {
    pub(crate) fn field_owner_can_read(&mut self, rule: &RuleExpr, owner: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        // Recipient witnesses are exact but are not the caller's observations.
        // Partition accounting prepays their schema-derived probe/step bound.
        // A recipient's witness deadline never enters the writer's answer: the scope is dropped, not emitted.
        let delivery_expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
        let result = self.authorized_scratch(self.rule_scratch(rule), &[], |runner, allowance| {
            let mut probe = DeliveryProbe { runner, known: true, allowance };
            let readable = rules::evaluate(rule, owner, None, row, false, &mut probe)?;
            Ok(readable && probe.known)
        });
        drop(delivery_expiry);
        result
    }

    pub(crate) fn prepay_field_delivery(&mut self, rule: &RuleExpr, slots: u64, maintained: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let probes = slots.saturating_mul(rules::rule_probes(rule));
        if maintained {
            self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::RuleProbes, probes)?;
            self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, slots)?;
        } else {
            self.meter.charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, probes)?;
            self.meter.step(slots)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub(super) fn authorize_fields(&self, table: &str, row: &Value, supplied: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table];
        let fields = supplied.as_object()?;
        let mut owned = false;
        let mut ordinary = false;
        for field in fields.keys().filter(|field| field.as_str() != "id") {
            if let Some(authority) = definition.written_by.get(field) {
                owned = true;
                if !self.context.is_system() && row.field(&authority.owner).ok() != Some(&Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone())) {
                    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
                    self.refusing_clause(table, &format!("field authority {field}"));
                    return Err(RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_FIELD_AUTHORITY", message: format!("{table}.{field} is written by .{}; omit {field} or write as the principal in .{} (guide rules)", authority.owner, authority.owner) });
                }
            } else { ordinary = true; }
        }
        Ok(owned && !ordinary)
    }

    pub(super) fn authorize_update_fields(&mut self, table: &str, old: &Value, next: &Value, patch: &Value, readable: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let owned_only = self.authorize_fields(table, old, patch).map_err(|error| if readable { error } else { self.no_write_target() })?;
        // An unrelated owner's omitted field is unknown, not its literal
        // default. It prevents prediction only when this transition needs it,
        // or when changing ownership would expose it to this device.
        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table];
        if self.device && definition.written_by.iter().any(|(field, authority)|
            !authority.audience && old.field(&authority.owner).ok() != Some(&Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone()))

    pub fn is_uniformly_readable(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Public { .. } | Self::Allow)
    }

    /// Every `.field` the rule reads, on any probe key or comparison.
    pub fn fields(&self, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {
        let term = |term: &RuleTerm, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>| {
            if let RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::Reference { column: field, .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column: field, .. } = term {
                out.insert(field.clone());
            }
        };
        match self {
            Self::Public { .. } | Self::Allow | Self::Deny => {}
            Self::Eq(left, right) | Self::Ne(left, right) | Self::Compare(_, left, right) => {
                term(left, out);
                term(right, out);
            }
            Self::And(items) | Self::Or(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| item.fields(out)),
            Self::Not(inner) => inner.fields(out),
            Self::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | Self::Created { key, equalities, .. } => {
                key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_, term)| term)).for_each(|item| term(item, out));
            }
        }
    }

    /// The disjuncts of the rule: an `or` flattens, anything else is itself.
    pub fn disjuncts(&self) -> Vec<&RuleExpr> {
        match self {
            Self::Or(items) => items.iter().flat_map(|item| item.disjuncts()).collect(),
            other => vec![other],
        }
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.6 — service seats read on the server and never enlarge a device's audience.
    pub fn service_grant(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        match self {
            Self::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(principal)))
            | Self::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(principal)), RuleTerm::Viewer)
                if principal.strip_prefix("service:").is_some_and(|name| !name.is_empty()) => Some(principal),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

pub(super) fn evaluate(expr:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<bool>{boolean(value(expr,row,rows,schema,viewer))}
fn value(e:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<Value>{Some(match e{
    Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"=>Value::Id(viewer.into()),
    Expr::Literal{value,..}=>match value{Literal::Str(s)=>Value::String(s.clone()),Literal::Bool(v)=>Value::Bool(*v),Literal::Null=>Value::Null,Literal::Number(s)=>Value::Int(s.parse().ok()?)},
    Expr::Focal{..}=>row.clone(),
    Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..})=>row.field(name).ok()?.clone(),
    Expr::Unary{op,value:x,..} if op=="not"=>Value::Bool(!boolean(value(x,row,rows,schema,viewer))?),
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="and"||op=="or"=>{
        let a=boolean(value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer));let b=boolean(value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer));
        let and=op=="and";let decisive=!and;
        Value::Bool(if a==Some(decisive)||b==Some(decisive){decisive}else if a.is_some()&&b.is_some(){and}else{return None;})
    },

        let a=value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;let b=value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
        if matches!(op.as_str(),"+"|"-"|"*") {
            let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b)else{return None;};
            let n=match op.as_str(){"+"=>i128::from(a)+i128::from(b),"-"=>i128::from(a)-i128::from(b),_=>i128::from(a)*i128::from(b)};
            return Value::int(n).ok();
        }
        // The binder contextually types principal/id literals. This partial
        // evaluator refuses that coercion rather than using host strings.
        if !matches!((&a,&b),(Value::Id(_),Value::Id(_))|(Value::String(_),Value::String(_))|(Value::Int(_),Value::Int(_))|(Value::Bool(_),Value::Bool(_))|(Value::Null,_)|(_,Value::Null)){return None;}

    },

        Value::int(sum).ok()?
    },

    Expr::Read(read)|Expr::Exists{read,..}=>{
