//! Field writes use the old owner; row transition rules still constrain them.
// @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — ownership grants only the explicitly touched owned fields.
use super::runner::Runner;
use super::rules::{self, Probe};
use crate::{error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError}, schema::{Effect, RuleExpr}, value::Value};

/// Delivery accounting must not make another recipient's unknown membership
/// a dependency of the writer's prediction. Server witnesses remain exact.
struct DeliveryProbe<'a, 'b> {
    runner: &'a mut Runner<'b>,
    known: bool,
    allowance: &'a super::Authorization,
}

impl Probe for DeliveryProbe<'_, '_> {
    fn take_changes(&mut self) -> Option<Vec<(i64, super::rules::Verdict)>> { self.runner.witness_changes.take() }
    fn replace_expiry(&mut self, expiry: Option<i64>) -> Option<i64> { self.runner.next_expiry.replace(expiry) }
    fn exists(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value], equalities: &[(String, Value)]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if !self.runner.read_charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, 1)? { return Ok(false); }
        let (present, known) = self.runner.probe_witness(self.allowance, table, index, key, equalities, false)?;
        self.known &= known;
        Ok(present)
    }

    fn created(&mut self, _table: &str, _index: &str, _key: &[Value]) -> bool { false }
}

impl<'r> Runner<'r> {
    pub(crate) fn field_owner_can_read(&mut self, rule: &RuleExpr, owner: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        // Recipient witnesses are exact but are not the caller's observations.
        // Partition accounting prepays their schema-derived probe/step bound.
        // A recipient's witness deadline never enters the writer's answer: the scope is dropped, not emitted.
        let delivery_expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
        let result = self.authorized_scratch(self.rule_scratch(rule), &[], |runner, allowance| {
            let mut probe = DeliveryProbe { runner, known: true, allowance };
            let readable = rules::evaluate(rule, owner, None, row, false, &mut probe)?;
            Ok(readable && probe.known)
        });
        drop(delivery_expiry);
        result
    }

    pub(crate) fn prepay_field_delivery(&mut self, rule: &RuleExpr, slots: u64, maintained: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let probes = slots.saturating_mul(rules::rule_probes(rule));
        if maintained {
            self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::RuleProbes, probes)?;
            self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, slots)?;
        } else {
            self.meter.charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, probes)?;
            self.meter.step(slots)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub(super) fn authorize_fields(&self, table: &str, row: &Value, supplied: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table];
        let fields = supplied.as_object()?;
        let mut owned = false;
        let mut ordinary = false;
        for field in fields.keys().filter(|field| field.as_str() != "id") {
            if let Some(authority) = definition.written_by.get(field) {
                owned = true;
                if !self.context.is_system() && row.field(&authority.owner).ok() != Some(&Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone())) {
                    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
                    self.refusing_clause(table, &format!("field authority {field}"));
                    return Err(RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_FIELD_AUTHORITY", message: format!("{table}.{field} is written by .{}; omit {field} or write as the principal in .{} (guide rules)", authority.owner, authority.owner) });
                }
            } else { ordinary = true; }
        }
        Ok(owned && !ordinary)
    }

    pub(super) fn authorize_update_fields(&mut self, table: &str, old: &Value, next: &Value, patch: &Value, readable: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let owned_only = self.authorize_fields(table, old, patch).map_err(|error| if readable { error } else { self.no_write_target() })?;
        // An unrelated owner's omitted field is unknown, not its literal
        // default. It prevents prediction only when this transition needs it,
        // or when changing ownership would expose it to this device.
        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table];
        if self.device && definition.written_by.iter().any(|(field, authority)|
            !authority.audience && old.field(&authority.owner).ok() != Some(&Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone()))
            && ((!owned_only || definition.update_transition) && definition.rules.update.needs_field_value(field, &definition.columns[field])
                || next.field(&authority.owner).ok() == Some(&Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone())))) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!("{table}: this transition needs another principal's private field; queue it for the server (guide rules)")));
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — a named transition is not permission to test guesses against someone else's private value.
        if !self.context.is_system() && let Some((field, authority)) = definition.written_by.iter().find(|(field, authority)|
            !authority.audience && old.field(&authority.owner).ok() != Some(&Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone()))
            && definition.rules.update.needs_field_value(field, &definition.columns[*field])) {
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            self.refusing_clause(table, &format!("field authority {field}"));
            return Err(if readable { RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_FIELD_AUTHORITY", message: format!("{table}.{field}: this transition needs the private value written by .{}; write as that owner or declare readable by audience (guide rules)", authority.owner) } } else { self.no_write_target() });
        }
        let transition = self.schema.tables[table].update_transition;
        if (!owned_only || transition) && !self.check_rule(table, Effect::Update, Some(old), next)? {
            return Err(if readable { RuntimeError::Rule { table: table.into(), effect: Effect::Update } } else { self.no_write_target() });
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub(crate) fn disclosed_row<'a>(&self, definition: &'a crate::schema::Table, row: Value) -> crate::value::Disclosed<'a> {
        match self.system_access() {
            Some(system) => definition.system_fields(row, &system),
            None => definition.project_fields(row, Some(&self.context.viewer)),
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn project_row(&mut self, table: &str, row: Value) -> Result<super::authorized_point::AuthorizedRow<'r>, RuntimeError> {
        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table];
        // A merge may retain previously authorized results after its next lane
        // cuts. Revalidate their authority internally without treating that

#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
pub fn seat_refusal(schema:&Schema,error:&RuntimeError,viewer:&str)->Refusal {
    let mut why=error.refusal();
    if viewer.starts_with("service:") && let RuntimeError::Rule {table,effect}|RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget {table,effect}=error {
        if let Some(def)=schema.table(table) {why.message.push_str(&format!("; {table} rule clause: {effect} <- {}",def.rules.for_effect(*effect).quarry()));}
    }
    why
}

        && catalog.maintains.get(&read.table).filter(|m| m.row_key.is_none()).and_then(|maintain| catalog.tables.get(&maintain.source)).is_some_and(|source| source.decl.expire.is_some() || source.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.expiring)) {
        return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_LIVE_MAINTAIN_PROBE", format!("a rule cannot probe {}.value because its source expires", read.table),
            "read the view in the program; its live value cannot be probed at one unit").guide("expire"));
    }
    if let Some(names) = &named {
        for ((_, term), (column, ty)) in terms.iter_mut().zip(names.iter().zip(&mut types)) {
            let expected = column_ty(target, column).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("{} has no column {column}", read.table), name_rewrite(column, target.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)))?;
            if !ty.comparable(&expected) && !(expected.is_identity() && matches!(term, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(_)))) { return Err(type_error(site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", ty.name(), read.table, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name()))); }
            if expected.required().is_identity() && let RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(text)) = term { *term = RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(text.clone())); *ty = Ty::Id; }
            if let (Ty::Enum(variants), RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(text))) = (expected.required(), &*term) {
                if !variants.contains(text) { return Err(type_error(site, format!("'{text}' is not one of {}.{column}'s variants {}", read.table, variants.join(", ")), format!("'{}'", variants[0]))); }
            }
        }
    }
    if named.is_none() {
        let typed = types.iter().cloned().zip(terms.iter().map(|(_, term)| matches!(term, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let declared = super::positional_keys::candidates(target, read, &typed, &catalog.synthesized);
        if declared.len() != 1 {
            return Err(super::positional_keys::refusal(target, read, &typed, &declared, catalog));
        }
        if read.index.is_none() {
            let mut selected = read.clone();
            selected.index = Some(declared[0].name.clone());
            return probe(catalog, info, &selected, site, bindings, extras);
        }
    }

//! Rule hops use the ordinary authorized point-read path under the rule's
//! prepaid allowance and the request's shared physical authorization limit.
use super::runner::Runner;
use crate::{error::RuntimeError, ir::{Expr, KeyProvenance, Read}, value::Value};
impl Runner<'_> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — final obligations resolve live hops at transaction
    // end, under the same prepaid/physical allowance as ordinary write rules.
    pub(super) fn final_reference_key(&mut self, source: &str, rule: &crate::schema::RuleExpr, row: &Value) -> Result<Option<Vec<Value>>, RuntimeError> {
        use crate::{schema::{RuleExpr, RuleTerm}, error::MeterKind};
        use super::{rules, read::RuleProbe};
        let RuleExpr::Exists { key, .. } = rule else { unreachable!() };
        let probes = key.iter().map(|term| term.references().count() as u64).sum();
        // The resolved key outlives the authorized read scratch. Reserve its
        // declared copy bound before probing, independent of hidden occupants.
        let retained = key.iter().fold(crate::rows::word_heap::slots::<Value>(key.len()), |bytes, term| {
            let (table, field) = match term {
                RuleTerm::Reference { .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { .. } => term.references().last().expect("validated"),
                RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::NextField(field) | RuleTerm::OldField(field) => (source,field.as_str()),
                RuleTerm::Viewer => return bytes.saturating_add(144),
                RuleTerm::Literal(value) => return bytes.saturating_add(super::runner::expression_copy(value)),
            };
            bytes.saturating_add(super::write_target::column_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[table].columns[field]))
        });
        self.meter.heap(retained)?;
        let viewer = self.context.viewer.clone();
        self.authorized_scratch(self.rule_scratch(rule), &[(MeterKind::RuleProbes, probes)], |runner, allowance| {
            let mut probe = RuleProbe { runner, allowance };
            let mut missing = false;
            let key = key.iter().map(|term| rules::term(term, &viewer, None, row, &mut probe, &mut missing)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
            Ok((!missing).then_some(key))
        })
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — absent, hidden and unheld parents fail closed,
    // including under negation; no raw witness field escapes authorization.
    pub(super) fn rule_reference(&mut self, table: &str, id: &Value, field: &str) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
        if !matches!(id, Value::Id(_)) { return Ok(None); }
        let read = Read { site: format!("rule {table}[ref].{field}"), table: table.into(), index: "byId".into(), key: vec![Expr::Literal(id.clone())], provenance: KeyProvenance::Computed };
        if self.device {
            let row = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.fetch(allowance, table, id.as_str()?))?;
            let Some(row) = row else {
                self.unknown_fact(table, "byId", &[id.clone()], &read.site);
                return Ok(None);
            };
            // Delivered history is readable data but cannot supply current write authority.
            if !self.witness_covered(table, "byId", &[id.clone()], &row)? {
                self.unknown_fact(table, "byId", &[id.clone()], &read.site);
                return Ok(None);
            }
            if !self.authorize_read(table, &row, false)? { return Ok(None); }
        }
        let row = self.get(&read)?;
        if row == Value::Null { return Ok(None); }
        if self.is_provisional(table, &row)? && self.schema.tables[table].sequences.contains_key(field) {
            self.provisional_sequence_read = true;
        }
        Ok(Some(row.field(field).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null)))
    }
}
    fn probe_witness_uncached(&mut self, allowance: &Authorization, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value], equalities: &[(String, Value)], current: bool) -> Result<(bool, bool), RuntimeError> {
        if self.schema.maintain_for(table).is_some_and(|m|m.row_key.is_some()) {
            let bytes = crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(&self.schema.tables[table])
                .saturating_add(crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as u64 * 4);
            let expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
            let result = self.authorized_scratch(bytes, &[(MeterKind::Examined,1)], |runner, scope| {
                    runner.probe_row_view(scope, table, index, key, equalities, current)
                });
            if matches!(result, Ok((true, true))) || equalities.iter().any(|(field, _)| field == "value") { expiry.emit(); }
            return result;
        }

        self.observe_sequence_selection(table, index, key)?;
        let definition = self.schema.table(table).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table}")))?;
        let index_def = definition.index(index).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing index {table}.{index}")))?;
        self.read_tables.insert(table.to_owned());
        self.aggregate_source_dependency(table);
        let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
        let mut live = false;
        let mut until: Option<i64> = None;
        let mut uncertain = false;
        loop {
            let (rows, limit) = if !definition.read_lifetime_sensitive() {
                // Non-expiring indexed predicates need only one candidate;
                // extra equalities bind a complete unique key.
                (self.live_lookup(allowance, table, index, index_def, key, #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] None)?.map(|row| (row, Vec::new())).into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(), usize::MAX,
                )
            } else { self.witness_scan(allowance, table, index, key, &bounds, 32)? };
            let count = rows.len();
            for (mut row, stored) in rows {
                bounds.after = self.witness_position(&stored, index_def.components.len() + 1, key.len())?;
                // @ref LLP 3000.036.003#2 — unopened witnesses are absent and
                // consume neither the public meter nor the authorization walk.
                if self.step_unopened(table, &row, &stored)? { continue; }
                self.witness_materialize(&mut row, definition)?;
                if self.inactive(table, &row)? || !equalities.iter().all(|(column, value)| row.field(column).unwrap_or(&Value::Null) == value) { continue; }
                // @ref LLP 3000.016#21a-owned-events-follow-their-source-rows — a held source whose event kind is not fully synced is no witness.
                if self.device && !crate::schema::timeline::holds_event(self.schema, table, &row) {
                    self.event_read_unknown(table, index, key, "rule probe");
                    uncertain = true;
                    continue;
                }
                if current && !self.witness_covered(table, index, key, &row)? { uncertain = true; continue; }
                let (present, known, expiry) = self.witness_lifetime(allowance, table, &row, current)?;
                uncertain |= !known;
                if !present { continue; }
                until = if live { until.zip(expiry).map(|(a, b)| a.max(b)) } else { expiry };
                live = true;
                // Finish the bounded enumeration even after a permanent witness.
                // Its position must not select success versus exhaustion.
            }
            if count < limit { break; }
    pub(in crate::engine) fn live_lookup(&mut self, allowance: &Authorization, table_name: &str, name: &str, index: &Index, key: &[Value], #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] mut encountered: Option<&mut bool>) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
        if crate::rows::disclosed::actual_size(table) && !self.device_work_active {
            let mark = self.meter.mark();
            let result = self.tx.scan_metered(allowance, table_name, name, key, &Bounds::default(), Dir::Asc, 1, &mut self.meter);
            self.meter.release_heap(mark);
            let Some((row,_)) = result?.pop() else { return Ok(None); };
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            if let Some(seen) = encountered.as_deref_mut() { *seen = true; }
            // @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — stored public bytes exclude adopted defaults.
            // Keep their materialization charge while the returned witness lives.
            let image = self.disclosed_row(table, row);
            // Public non-expiring images have authority before access. Enforce
            // the outer caller's ceiling even inside a restoring rule scope;
            // private witness work keeps its declaration-only admission.
            let ceiling = self.meter.ceilings.heap_bytes;
            let internal_heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
            if let Some((limit, used)) = self.caller_heap_ceiling {
                self.meter.ceilings.heap_bytes = limit;
                self.meter.used.heap_bytes = used;
            }
            let charged = crate::engine::authorized_scan::InternalLedger::with_meter(&mut self.meter, |meter, scope|
                image.charge_materialization_internal(meter, scope));
            if let Some((_, used)) = &mut self.caller_heap_ceiling {
                let public_heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
                self.meter.used.heap_bytes = internal_heap.saturating_add(public_heap.saturating_sub(*used));
                *used = public_heap;
            }
            self.meter.ceilings.heap_bytes = ceiling;
            charged?;
            return Ok(Some(image.into_value()));
        }
        if !table.read_lifetime_sensitive() && !self.device_work_active {
            return self.tx.lookup(allowance, table_name, name, index, key).map(|found| {
                    found.map(|mut row| {
                #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
                if let Some(seen) = encountered.as_deref_mut() { *seen = true; }
                materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table); row })
                });
        }
        let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
        loop {
            let (rows, limit) = self.witness_scan(allowance, table_name, name, key, &bounds, if !table.read_lifetime_sensitive() { 1 } else { 32 })?;
            let count = rows.len();
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            if count > 0 { if let Some(seen) = encountered.as_deref_mut() { *seen = true; } }
            for (mut row, stored) in rows {
                bounds.after = self.witness_position(&stored, index.components.len() + 1, key.len())?;
    }
}

/// The prefix expressions of a scan as `IndexKeyExpr`s, for callers that
/// pin values rather than expressions.
pub fn pinned_literals(prefix: &[Value]) -> Vec<IndexKeyExpr> {
    prefix.iter().map(|value| IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(Expr::Literal(value.clone()))).collect()
}

/// Something that can answer an `exists`/`created` probe while a rule runs.
pub trait Probe {
    fn reference(&mut self, _table: &str, _id: &Value, _field: &str) -> Result<Option<Value>, crate::error::RuntimeError> { Ok(None) }
    /// Exact count-predicate transitions, distinct from ordinary witness expiry.
    fn take_changes(&mut self) -> Option<Vec<(i64, super::rules::Verdict)>> { None }
    /// Exchange the private expiry accumulator; ordinary probes have none.
    fn replace_expiry(&mut self, _expiry: Option<i64>) -> Option<i64> { None }
    /// Coverage of the most recent leaf, consumed once by the shared evaluator.
    fn take_unknown(&mut self) -> bool { false }
    /// Called only when evaluation loads a field, after short-circuit decisions.
    fn field(&mut self, _field: &str, _old: bool) {}
    /// Apply the schema's point-key null rule before storage access. Timeline
    /// evaluation can visit operands that logical short-circuiting would skip.
    fn exists(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value], equalities: &[(String, Value)]) -> Result<bool, crate::error::RuntimeError>;
    fn created(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value]) -> bool;
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
pub enum Verdict { Readable, Denied, #[default] Unknown }

impl Verdict {
    fn not(self) -> Self {
        match self { Self::Readable => Self::Denied, Self::Denied => Self::Readable, Self::Unknown => Self::Unknown }
    }
    fn combine(self, other: Self, conjunction: bool) -> Self {
        let decisive = if conjunction { Self::Denied } else { Self::Readable };
        if self == decisive || other == decisive { decisive }
        else if self == Self::Unknown || other == Self::Unknown { Self::Unknown }
        else { decisive.not() }
    }
}

    }else{
        match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,context).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.state {
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{result,..}=>Ok(serde_json::json!({"sent":result})),
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{why,current}=>{
                // E_RULE can also mask authorization-budget exhaustion. Only
                // an authored require/constraint carries this rule marker;
                // bound/storage/schema/ambiguous engine refusals cannot be a
                // logical counterexample in our resource-excluding contract.
                if why.rule.is_none()||!matches!(why.family.as_str(),"rule"|"constraint") {
                    return Err(format!("resource/engine or ambiguous mutation refusal {} is outside paired logical comparison",why.code));
                }
                Ok(serde_json::json!({"refusal":{"code":why.code,"message":why.message},"current":current}))
            },
        }
    }
}

   349	fn fork(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
   350	    let store=fixture.store().fork_memory().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;

        let id = key.as_str()?;
        self.reserve_target_resolution(table_name, self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?, &patch, false)?;
        let Some(old) = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| {
            runner.fetch_write_target(allowance, table_name, id)
        })? else {

        self.replace(table_name, old, new, readable, &patch, copy)
    }

    pub(super) fn upsert(&mut self, table_name: &str, key: &Value, row: Supplied) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if self.device && self.schema.tables[table_name].not_before.is_some() {
        fn term(term: &RuleTerm) -> String {
            match term {
                RuleTerm::Reference { table, column, field, old } => format!("{table}[{}.{column}].{field}", if *old { "old" } else { "" }),
                RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column, hops, old } => hops.iter().fold(format!("{}.{column}", if *old { "old" } else { "" }), |key, hop| format!("{}[{key}].{}", hop.table, hop.field)),
                RuleTerm::Field(f) => format!(".{f}"),
                RuleTerm::OldField(f) => format!("old.{f}"),
                RuleTerm::NextField(f) => format!("next.{f}"),
                RuleTerm::Viewer => "viewer".to_owned(),
                RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(s)) => format!("'{s}'"),
                RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(s)) => format!("'{s}'"),
                RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Bool(b)) => b.to_string(), RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(n)) => n.to_string(), RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null) => "null".to_owned(), // Quarry, never `{"Bool":true}`
                RuleTerm::Literal(v) => crate::value::canonical_json(v),
            }
        }
        match self {
            Self::Public { .. } => "public".to_owned(),
            Self::Allow => "allow".to_owned(),
            Self::Deny => "deny".to_owned(),
            Self::Eq(l, r) => format!("{} = {}", term(l), term(r)),
            Self::Compare(op,l,r) => format!("{} {} {}", term(l), match op { crate::ir::BinaryOp::Gte => ">=", crate::ir::BinaryOp::Gt => ">", crate::ir::BinaryOp::Lte => "<=", _ => "<" }, term(r)),
            Self::Ne(l, r) => format!("{} != {}", term(l), term(r)),
            Self::And(items) => items.iter().map(|i| if matches!(i,Self::Or(_)){format!("({})",i.quarry())}else{i.quarry()}).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and "),
            Self::Or(items) => items.iter().map(|i| i.quarry()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" or "),
            Self::Not(inner) => format!("not ({})", inner.quarry()),
            Self::Exists { table, key, equalities, .. } => format!("exists {table}[{}]", key.iter().map(term).chain(equalities.iter().map(|(col, value)| format!("{col} = {}", term(value)))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),
            Self::Created { table, key, equalities, .. } => format!("created {table}[{}]", key.iter().map(term).chain(equalities.iter().map(|(col, value)| format!("{col} = {}", term(value)))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),
        }
    }
}

//! Column worlds execute authored observers, including derived/count/refusal paths.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#121-column-read-intent — excluding a field from a projection must not produce a synthetic whole-row refutation.
use super::*;
fn alternative(kind:&C,old:&Value)->Option<Value>{Some(match (kind.required(),old){
    (C::Text{..},Value::String(s))=>Value::String(s.chars().map(|c|if c=='y'{'x'}else{'y'}).collect()),
    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|s|Value::String((*s).clone())!=*old)?.clone()),
    (C::Principal,Value::Id(s))=>Value::Id(if s==VIEWER{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),
    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(n.checked_add(1)?),
    (C::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(if n<max{n+1}else if n>min{n-1}else{return None}),
    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
    _=>return None,
})}
fn sample_rows(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,inv:&Invariant,row:&Value)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>{
    let column=inv.column.as_ref()?;let kind=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(column)?;
    let alt=alternative(kind,row.field(column).ok()?)?;
    let mut out=rows.clone();
    let selected=out.get_mut(&inv.table)?.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())?;
    if let Value::Row(fields)=selected{fields.insert(column.clone(),alt);}else{return None;}
    Some(out)
}
fn run_pair(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Some(other)=sample_rows(bound,rows,inv,row)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column pair needs a second typed non-asset value",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or("")));return;};
    let altrow=other[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
    let mut actors=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for r in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=r.field(c)&&!actors.contains(p){actors.push(p.clone());}}}}
    for viewer in actors{
        if policy(bound,inv,rows,&viewer,None,row)!=Ok(false)||policy(bound,inv,&other,&viewer,None,altrow)!=Ok(false){continue;}
        for bp in &bound.programs{
            let p=&bp.program;
            if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native{continue;}
            let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
            if json.contains("NewId")||json.contains("Emit"){continue;}
            let mut args_list=histories::argument_cases(&p.args,out);
            for args in &mut args_list{for (name,kind) in &p.args{if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(r)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())&&let Ok(id)=r.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}}}
            for args in args_list{
                let execute=|alternate:bool|->Result<serde_json::Value,String>{
                    let seeds=if alternate{&other}else{rows};
                    let mut e=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{
                        let mut e=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())?;
                        if alternate{
                            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            let Value::Row(fields)=altrow else{return Err("not a row".into());};
                            let clean=Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect());
                            e.seed_at([(inv.table.clone(),clean)],NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
                        }e
                    }else{engine(bound,seeds,program("$column.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))?};
                    let actual=readback(&e)?;
                    let current=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).ok_or("column target is absent after validation")?;
                    if policy(bound,inv,&actual,&viewer,None,current)!=Ok(false){return Err("validated column-world policy is not false".into());}
                    if alternate&&fixture.is_some(){
                        for (table,rs) in rows.iter().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()){
                            let definition=&bound.schema.tables[table];
                            let same=rs.iter().all(|old|actual.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|definition.columns.keys().filter(|c|!definition.maintained.contains_key(*c)&&!(table==&inv.table&&inv.column.as_ref()==Some(*c)&&old.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())).all(|c|old.field(c).ok()==new.field(c).ok())));
                            if !same{return Err(format!("alternate seed changes non-protected base fields in {table}; capture/recorded reduction unavailable"));}
                        }
                    }
                    paired::observe(&mut e,p,&viewer,args.clone())
                };
                let (a,b)=match (execute(false),execute(true)){
                    (Ok(a),Ok(b))=>(a,b),
                    (Err(why),_)|(_,Err(why))=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{} / {}: declined column world: {why}",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),p.name));continue;}
                };
                out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
                if a==b{continue;}
                if !matches!((execute(false),execute(true)),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if aa==a&&bb==b){continue;}
                out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:if fixture.is_some(){Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace}else{Reachability::SyntheticOperation},invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer,operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"column read observation".into(),row:row.clone(),old:Some(altrow.clone()),supporting_rows:rows.clone(),history:Vec::new(),rule_path:format!("authored observer depends on {}.{} while policy is false in both typed worlds",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()),scope:if fixture.is_some(){"primary observation executes on trusted fixture; alternate column is a dependency test, not an authored alternate-history claim"}else{"schema-valid synthetic worlds; authored observer, no application reachability claim"}.into(),observations:Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:other.clone()})});
                return;
            }
        }
    }
}
pub(super) fn generated(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_some()&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))){
        for variant in 0..16{
            let rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column snapshot: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let e=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$column.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column seed: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
            for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,None,out);}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){break;}
        }
    }
}
pub(super) fn fixture(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(rows)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_some()){
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),out);}
    }
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],None),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            let committed=steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()>=committed){continue;}
            if policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let Ok(id)=row.field("id").and_then(Value::as_str)else{continue;};
            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete(&inv.table,id){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if policy(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!p.program.native){
                let p=&bp.program;
                for args in fixture_cases(&p.args,rows,actors,out).into_iter().take(32){
                    out.attempted+=2;
                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
                    if a==b{continue;}
                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){continue;}
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
                    if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
                    continue 'actors;
                }
            }
        }}
    }
}
fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in &bound.invariants{
        if inv.target=="read"||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
            if old==Some(row){continue;}
            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
            if inv.target!="always"&&inv.operation.is_none()&&!super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
            if inv.target=="always" {
                if policy(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
            }else{
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                if policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old)==Ok(false)){
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty(){return;}
    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    if initial.values().all(Vec::is_empty){out.unsupported.insert("history needs a trusted fixture; generated snapshots have separate reachability labels".into());return;}
    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
    let probes=Vec::new();
    let mut groups=Vec::new();
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,&initial,&actors,out);
        let mut group=Vec::new();
        for args in cases{for viewer in &actors{group.push((&bp.program,viewer.clone(),args.clone()));}}
        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
    }
    let mut candidates=Vec::new();
    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){candidates.push(c.clone());}}}
    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
    // Breadth first, then diagonal pairs: no first step monopolizes the cap.
    let singles=candidates.len().min(LIMIT);
    for n in 0..singles+LIMIT {
        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let k=n-singles;vec![k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()]};
        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
        for i in indices{
            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
                Ok(result)=>{
                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);}
                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
            }
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
}
//! Advice identifies a risk shape; it never supplies a proof assumption.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#6-bakeoff-repairs — current roles, grant writers, required evidence and refusal oracles.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramKind};
fn rule_parts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(rule_parts).collect(),RuleExpr::Not(x)=>rule_parts(x),r=>vec![r]}}
fn role_test(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {rule_parts(rule).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..} if key.iter().any(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&equalities.iter().any(|(_,t)|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Literal(_)))))}
fn reader_columns(rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{rule_parts(rule).into_iter().filter_map(|r|match r{RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))=>Some(f.clone()),_=>None}).collect()}
fn note(notes:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,rewrite:&str){notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,rewrite).guide("security"));}
fn mutable_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool {
    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(name)else{return false;};
    let roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
    if roles.is_empty(){return false;}
    let mutable=roles.iter().any(|c|!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
    mutable&&bound.programs.iter().any(|p|snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==name&&matches!(*kind,"update"|"upsert"|"delete")))
}

/// `schedule name every 2m [at 08:00]: job`. Period boundaries are whole
/// multiples of `every_ms` since the epoch, shifted by `offset_ms`; the job
/// runs once for each boundary the server's clock crosses, with the period
/// that ended there as `periodStart`/`periodEnd` when it declares them.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Schedule {
    pub name: String,
    pub every_ms: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub offset_ms: u64,
    pub target: String,
    /// Actions require an explicit `service:<name>`; jobs remain system work.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub service: Option<String>,
    /// `every N months` (years are 12N): boundaries at the start of each UTC
    /// month whose ordinal since 1970-01 is a multiple of N, plus `offset_ms`
    /// (day and time of day, under 28 days). A calendar schedule has
    /// `every_ms = 0`; zero keeps a fixed period and its serialized bytes.
    // @ref LLP 3000.040#24-monthly-and-yearly-schedules — an older engine refuses every_ms = 0 at load.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "schedule::is_zero_months")]

        self.constraints.iter().find_map(|constraint| match constraint {
            Constraint::Cap { by: group, max } if group == by && !self.cap_exempts_null(group) => Some(*max),
            _ => None,
        })
    }

    /// A cap grouping by a `set null` reference does not count its null rows
    /// (LLP 3000.039 §2.2), so it proves no population bound.
    pub fn cap_exempts_null(&self, by: &[String]) -> bool {
        by.iter().any(|column| self.on_delete.get(column) == Some(&references::OnDelete::SetNull))
    }

    pub fn immutable_fields(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &str> {
        self.constraints.iter().flat_map(|constraint| match constraint {
            Constraint::Immutable { fields } => fields.iter().map(String::as_str).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            _ => Vec::new(),
        })
    }

    /// Whether `row` is past its `expire` column at `now`: absent to every
    /// reader, pending the sweep. A null time never expires.
    pub fn expired(&self, row: &Value, now: i64) -> bool {
        self.deadline(row).is_some_and(|at| at <= now)
    }

    /// The index the expiry sweep walks: the first leading with the
    /// `expire` column, as the declaration requires.
    pub fn expiry_index(&self) -> Option<(&str, &Index)> {
        let column = self.expiry_column()?;
        self.indexes.iter().find(|(_, index)| {
                index.is_plain() && index.columns().first().map(String::as_str) == Some(column)
            }).map(|(name, index)| (name.as_str(), index))
    }

    /// The column carrying the principal a `sync to T[...]` audience reads.
    pub fn principal_columns(&self) -> Vec<&str> {
        self.columns.iter().filter(|(_, kind)| matches!(kind, ColumnType::Principal)).map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect()
    }
}

    #[default]

struct Snapshot<'a>{schema:&'a Schema,rows:&'a BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>}
impl Probe for Snapshot<'_>{
    fn exists(&mut self,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[Value],equalities:&[(String,Value)])->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{
        let t=&self.schema.tables[table];let Some(idx)=t.index(index)else{return Ok(false)};
        if idx.components.len()!=key.len(){return Ok(false);}
        if idx.unique&&idx.components.iter().any(|c|c.column().is_some_and(|n|t.columns.get(n).is_some_and(C::is_optional)))&&key.contains(&Value::Null){return Ok(false);}
        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|!t.expired(r,NOW)).any(|r|idx.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(c,v)|c.column().is_some_and(|n|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v)))&&equalities.iter().all(|(n,v)|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v))))
    }
    fn created(&mut self,_:&str,_:&str,_:&[Value])->bool{false}
    // Policies describe raw independent intent. The observer itself must still
    // execute through the engine's live/authorized reference implementation.
    fn reference(&mut self,table:&str,id:&Value,field:&str)->Result<Option<Value>,RuntimeError>{
        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(id)).and_then(|r|r.field(field).ok()).cloned())
    }
}
fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->Result<bool,String>{
    
    let Some(p)=inv.policy.as_ref() else{return inv.syntax.as_ref().and_then(|e|super::concrete::evaluate(e,row,rows,&bound.schema,viewer)).ok_or_else(||"concrete policy expression is unknown/unsupported (including clock and typed literal coercions)".into());};
    
    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    rules::evaluate(p,viewer,old,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn program(name:&str,kind:ProgramKind,body:Vec<Stmt>)->Program{Program{name:name.into(),kind,args:BTreeMap::new(),body,read_time_granularity_ms:None,returned_shapes:BTreeMap::new(),native:false}}
fn engine(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probe:Program)->Result<Engine,String>{
    engine_with(bound,rows,vec![probe])
}
fn engine_with(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
    let store=Store::memory().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    }}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn argument_cases(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    fixture_cases(args,&BTreeMap::new(),&[VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into()],out)
}

**NOT READY.** Material issues remain in the current files:

1. **Column mutations can produce false reachable violations.** [column_reads.rs:49](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/column_reads.rs:49) evaluates policy before executing the observer. A permitted mutation can transfer ownership to the caller, then return the now-authorized column. Different outputs across column worlds are nevertheless labeled `AuthoredFixtureTrace`. **Repair:** establish policy at the disclosure boundary; until tracing exists, decline mutations that can change policy fields or dependencies.

2. **Snapshot probes still count unopened rows.** [challenges.rs:83](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges.rs:83) now excludes expired rows, but ignores `not before`. For a policy and read rule both saying `not exists bans[viewer]`, a future ban makes the snapshot policy false while the engine correctly treats the ban as absent. A column observer can therefore falsely fail acceptance. **Repair:** implement exact live-witness semantics, or report unsupported populations without refuting.

3. **Fixture loading and campaign execution use different clocks.** [testing.rs:65](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/testing.rs:65) resolves fixtures against wall time; security forks and operations use fixed `NOW`. `check` instead loads fixtures directly at that fixed clock. Thus `ends: "now+1h"` can be live under `check` and expired during `test`’s security campaign. The absent-world `seed_delete` also reads wall time. **Repair:** use one explicit campaign clock throughout loading, forking, policy evaluation, operations and paired deletion.

4. **Reachable deletes are never evaluated.** [histories.rs:95](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/histories.rs:95) iterates only surviving rows and classifies changes solely as insert/update. An authored unauthorized delete can execute successfully without producing a reachable delete-policy violation; its synthetic witness remains non-failing. **Repair:** inspect removed pre-images and judge direct delete/operation assertions against the pre-write state. Keep ambiguous implicit deletions unsupported.

5. **Column privacy is skipped after history steps.** [histories.rs:60](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/histories.rs:60) explicitly excludes column-read invariants. The separate column campaign observes only the initial fixture. Consequently, a demotion that leaves a retained grant can expose a protected column without a reachable detection. **Repair:** run column-world observers after each committed history step and preserve the authored prefix in the witness.

6. **Column arguments still select only the first reference row.** [column_reads.rs:33](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/column_reads.rs:33) generates arguments without fixture rows, then replaces required references with `rs.first()`. Optional references retain fabricated candidates. Leaks involving later fixture rows are missed. **Repair:** use `fixture_cases` with the full fixture and actor set, under reported bounds.

7. **Grant-lifetime advice misses deletion-based revocation.** [lints.rs:13](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-sec3/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/lints.rs:13) requires a mutable role field even when an authored operation deletes role rows. An immutable role remains revocable through deletion, so retained grants can outlive it without advice. **Repair:** treat admitted deletion as revocation independently of field immutability; inspect actual role-field writes for update/upsert mutability.

Read-only source review; no files edited, other reviews read, or tests run. This verdict makes no all-highs or no-false-refusal qualification claim.
